### [1] [Latent-Compressed Variational Autoencoder for Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16479)
*Jiarui Guan,Wenshuai Zhao,Zhengtao Zou,Juho Kannala,Arno Solin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种视频潜在压缩方法，通过移除潜在表示中的高频分量而非直接减少通道数，在保持相同压缩比的同时获得更好的视频重建质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频变分自编码器通常需要大量潜在通道来保证高质量重建，但过多的通道会阻碍潜在扩散模型的收敛并降低生成性能，即使重建质量仍然很高

Method: 提出一种潜在压缩方法，移除视频潜在表示中的高频分量，而不是直接减少通道数（后者通常会损害重建保真度）

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在保持相同总体压缩比的情况下，相比强基线实现了更优的视频重建质量

Conclusion: 通过移除高频分量而非减少通道数来进行潜在压缩，能够在保持压缩效率的同时改善视频重建质量，解决了潜在扩散模型中通道数过多的问题

Abstract: Video variational autoencoders (VAEs) used in latent diffusion models typically require a sufficiently large number of latent channels to ensure high-quality video reconstruction. However, recent studies have revealed that an excessive number of latent channels can impede the convergence of latent diffusion models and deteriorate their generative performance, even when reconstruction quality remains high. We propose a latent compression method that removes high-frequency components in video latent representations rather than directly reducing the number of channels, which often compromises reconstruction fidelity. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves superior video reconstruction quality compared to strong baselines while maintaining the same overall compression ratio.

</details>


### [2] [Erasing Thousands of Concepts: Towards Scalable and Practical Concept Erasure for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16481)
*Hoigi Seo,Byung Hyun Lee,Jaehyun Cho,Sungjin Lim,Se Young Chun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ETC框架通过t分布混合模型建模概念分布，使用MoEraser模块实现大规模概念擦除，能在擦除数千个概念的同时保持生成质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除方法在可扩展性、精确性和鲁棒性之间存在平衡问题，通常只能擦除几百个概念，无法满足大规模安全需求

Method: 1) 使用t分布混合模型建模低秩概念分布；2) 通过仿射最优传输实现目标概念的精确定位擦除；3) 训练MoEraser模块移除目标嵌入同时保留锚定嵌入；4) 通过向文本嵌入投影器注入噪声并微调MoEraser实现鲁棒性

Result: 在超过2000个跨异质领域的概念和扩散模型上的实验表明，该方法在大规模概念擦除方面达到了最先进的可扩展性和精确性

Conclusion: ETC框架成功解决了大规模概念擦除的挑战，实现了数千个概念的高效擦除，同时保持了生成质量和对白盒攻击的鲁棒性

Abstract: Large-scale text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models deliver remarkable visual fidelity but pose safety risks due to their capacity to reproduce undesirable content, such as copyrighted ones. Concept erasure has emerged as a mitigation strategy, yet existing approaches struggle to balance scalability, precision, and robustness, which restricts their applicability to erasing only a few hundred concepts. To address these limitations, we present Erasing Thousands of Concepts (ETC), a scalable framework capable of erasing thousands of concepts while preserving generation quality. Our method first models low-rank concept distributions via a Student's t-distribution Mixture Model (tMM). It enables pin-point erasure of target concepts via affine optimal transport while preserving others by anchoring the boundaries of target concept distributions without pre-defined anchor concepts. We then train a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-based module, termed MoEraser, which removes target embeddings while preserving the anchor embeddings. By injecting noise into the text embedding projector and fine-tuning MoEraser for recovery, our framework achieves robustness to white-box attack such as module removal. Extensive experiments on over 2,000 concepts across heterogeneous domains and diffusion models demerate state-of-the-art scalability and precision in large-scale concept erasure.

</details>


### [3] [Dynamic Eraser for Guided Concept Erasure in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16483)
*Qinghui Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DSS是一种无需训练的轻量级框架，通过敏感语义边界建模和敏感语义引导实现可控概念擦除，在保持输出保真度的同时达到91.0%的平均擦除率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I扩散模型的概念擦除方法存在局限性：特征修正方法容易导致不受控制的过度修正，词元级干预方法难以处理语义粒度和上下文，且两种方法都容易出现严重的语义漂移甚至完全表示崩溃。

Method: 提出动态语义引导(DSS)框架，包含两个核心组件：1)敏感语义边界建模(SSBM)自动发现安全语义锚点；2)敏感语义引导(SSG)利用交叉注意力特征进行精确检测，并通过基于良好定义目标推导出的闭式解进行修正。

Result: DSS实现了91.0%的平均擦除率，显著优于SOTA方法（从18.6%到85.9%），同时对输出保真度影响最小。

Conclusion: DSS提供了一种轻量级、无需训练、可解释且可控的概念擦除框架，能有效抑制敏感内容同时保留良性语义，解决了现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: Concept erasure in Text-To-Image (T2I) diffusion models is vital for safe content generation, but existing inference-time methods face significant limitations. Feature-correction approaches often cause uncontrolled over-correction, while token-level interventions struggle with semantic granularity and context. Moreover, both types of methods are prone to severe semantic drift or even complete representation collapse. To address these challenges, we present Dynamic Semantic Steering (DSS), a lightweight, training-free framework for interpretable and controllable concept erasure. DSS introduces: 1) Sensitive Semantic Boundary Modeling (SSBM) to automate the discovery of safe semantic anchors, and 2) Sensitive Semantic Guidance (SSG), which leverages cross-attention features for precise detection and performs correction via a closed-form solution derived from a well-posed objective. This ensures optimal suppression of sensitive content while preserving benign semantics. DSS achieves an average erasure rate of 91.0\%, significantly outperforming SOTA methods (from 18.6\% to 85.9\%) with minimal impact on output fidelity.

</details>


### [4] [DexWorldModel: Causal Latent World Modeling towards Automated Learning of Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16484)
*Yueci Deng,Guiliang Liu,Kui Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLWM通过使用DINOv3特征作为生成目标来解耦交互语义与视觉噪声，采用双状态测试时训练内存实现O(1)内存占用，并通过推测异步推理减少延迟，最终在复杂双臂仿真和零样本sim-to-real迁移中取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式世界-动作模型在部署时面临三个主要瓶颈：冗余的像素级重建导致计算开销大、O(T)内存缩放限制长时程任务、顺序推理延迟影响实时性，这些因素严重制约了机器人操作任务的部署效率。

Method: 1. 使用DINOv3特征作为生成目标，解耦交互语义与视觉噪声；2. 双状态测试时训练内存(TTT Memory)确保O(1)内存占用；3. 推测异步推理(SAI)将部分扩散去噪过程与物理执行并行化；4. EmbodiChain在线框架通过注入无限流物理基础轨迹建立效率定律。

Result: 在复杂双臂仿真中取得最先进性能，在物理机器人上实现前所未有的零样本sim-to-real迁移，性能优于在真实数据上显式微调的基线方法，推测异步推理将阻塞延迟减少约50%。

Conclusion: CLWM通过语义特征生成、恒定内存占用和异步推理优化，有效解决了生成式世界模型在机器人操作中的部署瓶颈，为高效、可扩展的机器人策略学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Deploying generative World-Action Models for manipulation is severely bottlenecked by redundant pixel-level reconstruction, $\mathcal{O}(T)$ memory scaling, and sequential inference latency. We introduce the Causal Latent World Model (CLWM), which employs DINOv3 features as generative targets to disentangle interaction semantics from visual noise, yielding highly robust domain generalization. To overcome memory scaling, CLWM features a Dual-State Test-Time Training (TTT) Memory that guarantees a strict $\mathcal{O}(1)$ footprint for long-horizon tasks. To overcome deployment latency, we propose Speculative Asynchronous Inference (SAI) to mask partial diffusion denoising behind physical execution, cutting blocking latency by about $50\%$. To scale robust policies, we present EmbodiChain, an online framework that establishes the Efficiency Law by injecting an infinite flow of physics-grounded trajectories during training. Extensive experiments validate that CLWM achieves state-of-the-art performance in complex dual-arm simulation and unprecedented zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on physical robots, outperforming baselines explicitly finetuned on real-world data.

</details>


### [5] [Geometry-Aware CLIP Retrieval via Local Cross-Modal Alignment and Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16487)
*Nirmalendu Prakash,Narmeen Fatimah Oozeer,Xin Su,Phillip Howard,Shaan Shah,Zoe Wanying He,Shuang Wu,Shivam Raval,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Meenakshi Khosla,Amir Abdullah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过邻域对齐改进CLIP检索，引入匈牙利匹配的邻域级重排序和查询条件化的局部引导，以解决局部几何不一致问题，无需重新训练即可提升检索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP检索通常被视为共享嵌入空间中的点相似度问题，虽然实现了强大的全局跨模态对齐，但许多检索失败源于局部几何不一致：邻近项被错误排序，导致系统性混淆（如五边形与六边形）并产生扩散、弱控制的结果集。

Method: 提出两种方法：1) 通过匈牙利匹配进行邻域级重排序，奖励结构一致性；2) 查询条件化的局部引导，利用查询周围对比邻域推导的方向重塑检索。

Result: 这些技术在属性绑定和组合检索任务上提高了检索性能。两种方法都作用于局部邻域但扮演不同角色：重排序奖励对齐，而局部引导控制邻域结构。

Conclusion: 检索质量和可控性关键依赖于局部结构，可以在推理时利用这些结构而无需重新训练，展示了邻域对齐方法对改进CLIP检索的有效性。

Abstract: CLIP retrieval is typically framed as a pointwise similarity problem in a shared embedding space. While CLIP achieves strong global cross-modal alignment, many retrieval failures arise from local geometric inconsistencies: nearby items are incorrectly ordered, leading to systematic confusions (e.g., pentagon vs. hexagon) and produces diffuse, weakly controlled result sets. Prior work largely optimizes for point wise relevance or finetuning to mitigate these problems. We instead view retrieval as a problem of neighborhood alignment. Our work introduces (1) neighborhood-level re-ranking via Hungarian matching, which rewards structural consistency; (2) query-conditioned local steering, where directions derived from contrastive neighborhoods around the query reshape retrieval. We show that these techniques improve retrieval performance on attribute-binding and compositional retrieval tasks. Together, these methods operate on local neighborhoods but serve different roles: re-ranking rewards alignment whereas local steering controls neighborhood structure. This shows that retrieval quality and controllability depend critically on local structure, which can be exploited at inference time without retraining.

</details>


### [6] [An Uncertainty-Aware Loss Function Incorporating Fuzzy Logic: Application to MRI Brain Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16490)
*Hanuman Verma,Akshansh Gupta,Pranabesh Maji,Saurav Mandal,Vijay Kumar Pandey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合模糊逻辑的新型损失函数，用于处理脑MRI图像分割中的不确定性，通过整合分类交叉熵和模糊熵来改善分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑MRI图像分割在神经疾病诊断和医学图像计算中至关重要，但现有深度学习方法在处理分割不确定性方面存在不足。需要开发能够有效处理像素分类固有不确定性的损失函数。

Method: 提出一种新颖的损失函数，将传统的分类交叉熵（CCE）损失函数与基于模糊逻辑的模糊熵相结合。该损失函数利用模糊逻辑来处理像素分类中的不确定性，并在U-Net和U-Net++架构上进行了评估。

Result: 在IBSR和OASIS两个公开基准数据集上的实验结果表明，使用所提损失函数训练的模型在多个性能指标上均优于仅使用CCE损失函数的模型，同时能有效处理训练过程中的不确定性。

Conclusion: 所提出的模糊逻辑集成损失函数不仅提高了脑图像分割的性能，还增强了模型预测的可靠性，为处理医学图像分割中的不确定性提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate brain image segmentation, particularly for distinguishing various tissues from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) images, plays a pivotal role in finding the neurological dis ease and medical image computing. In deep learning approaches, loss functions are very crucial for optimizing the model. In this study, we introduce a novel loss function integrating fuzzy logic to deals uncertainty issues in brain image segmentation into various tissues. It integrates the well-known categorical cross-entropy (CCE) loss function and fuzzy entropy based on fuzzy logic. By employing fuzzy logic, this loss function accounts for the inherent uncertainties in pixel classifications. The proposed loss function has been evaluated on two publicly available benchmark datasets, IBSR and OASIS, using two widely recognised architectures, U-Net and U-Net++. Experimental results demonstrate that the trained model with proposed loss function provided better results in comparison to the CCE optimisation function in terms of various performance metrics. Additionally, it effectively enhances segmentation performance while handling meaningful uncer tainty during training. The findings suggest that this approach not only improves segmentation outcomes but also contributes to the reliability of model predictions.

</details>


### [7] [A Lightweight Transformer for Pain Recognition from Brain Activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16491)
*Stefanos Gkikas,Christian Arzate Cruz,Yu Fang,Lu Cao,Muhammad Umar Khan,Thomas Kassiotis,Giorgos Giannakakis,Raul Fernandez Rojas,Randy Gomez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级Transformer架构，通过统一token化机制融合多种fNIRS表征，实现互补信号视图的联合建模，在保持计算紧凑性的同时获得竞争性疼痛识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疼痛是多方面且普遍的现象，具有显著的临床和社会负担，因此可靠的自动化评估成为关键目标。现有方法在处理多模态fNIRS信号时通常需要模态特定的适应或增加架构复杂性。

Method: 提出轻量级Transformer架构，通过统一token化机制融合多种fNIRS表征（原始波形和功率谱密度）。采用结构化分割方案控制局部聚合和全局交互的粒度，将异构输入投影到共享潜在表示中，保留空间、时间和时频特性。

Result: 在AI4Pain数据集上评估，使用堆叠的原始波形和功率谱密度fNIRS输入。实验结果显示竞争性的疼痛识别性能，同时保持计算紧凑性，适合GPU和CPU硬件的实时推理。

Conclusion: 该方法通过统一token化机制有效融合多fNIRS表征，在保持轻量级架构的同时实现竞争性疼痛识别性能，为实时疼痛评估提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Pain is a multifaceted and widespread phenomenon with substantial clinical and societal burden, making reliable automated assessment a critical objective. This paper presents a lightweight transformer architecture that fuses multiple fNIRS representations through a unified tokenization mechanism, enabling joint modeling of complementary signal views without requiring modality-specific adaptations or increasing architectural complexity. The proposed token-mixing strategy preserves spatial, temporal, and time-frequency characteristics by projecting heterogeneous inputs onto a shared latent representation, using a structured segmentation scheme to control the granularity of local aggregation and global interaction. The model is evaluated on the AI4Pain dataset using stacked raw waveform and power spectral density representations of fNIRS inputs. Experimental results demonstrate competitive pain recognition performance while remaining computationally compact, making the approach suitable for real-time inference on both GPU and CPU hardware.

</details>


### [8] [HQA-VLAttack: Towards High Quality Adversarial Attack on Vision-Language Pre-Trained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16499)
*Han Liu,Jiaqi Li,Zhi Xu,Xiaotong Zhang,Xiaoming Xu,Fenglong Ma,Yuanman Li,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HQA-VLAttack：一种针对视觉语言预训练模型的黑盒对抗攻击框架，通过文本和图像两阶段攻击生成高质量对抗样本，显著提升攻击成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对视觉语言预训练模型的黑盒对抗攻击方法存在两个主要问题：1）依赖复杂的迭代交叉搜索策略，查询开销大；2）仅考虑降低正样本对的相似度，忽略了负样本对相似度的优化，影响攻击效果

Method: 提出两阶段攻击框架：1）文本攻击阶段使用counter-fitting词向量生成替代词集，保证语义一致性；2）图像攻击阶段先通过层重要性引导策略初始化对抗样本，再利用对比学习优化对抗扰动，同时降低正样本对相似度并提高负样本对相似度

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验结果表明，HQA-VLAttack在攻击成功率方面显著优于现有基线方法

Conclusion: HQA-VLAttack是一个简单有效的视觉语言预训练模型黑盒对抗攻击框架，通过同时优化正负样本对的相似度，在保证语义一致性的前提下显著提升了攻击性能

Abstract: Black-box adversarial attack on vision-language pre-trained models is a practical and challenging task, as text and image perturbations need to be considered simultaneously, and only the predicted results are accessible. Research on this problem is in its infancy, and only a handful of methods are available. Nevertheless, existing methods either rely on a complex iterative cross-search strategy, which inevitably consumes numerous queries, or only consider reducing the similarity of positive image-text pairs but ignore that of negative ones, which will also be implicitly diminished, thus inevitably affecting the attack performance. To alleviate the above issues, we propose a simple yet effective framework to generate high-quality adversarial examples on vision-language pre-trained models, named HQA-VLAttack, which consists of text and image attack stages. For text perturbation generation, it leverages the counter-fitting word vector to generate the substitute word set, thus guaranteeing the semantic consistency between the substitute word and the original word. For image perturbation generation, it first initializes the image adversarial example via the layer-importance guided strategy, and then utilizes contrastive learning to optimize the image adversarial perturbation, which ensures that the similarity of positive image-text pairs is decreased while that of negative image-text pairs is increased. In this way, the optimized adversarial images and texts are more likely to retrieve negative examples, thereby enhancing the attack success rate. Experimental results on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that HQA-VLAttack significantly outperforms strong baselines in terms of attack success rate.

</details>


### [9] [From Handwriting to Structured Data: Benchmarking AI Digitisation of Handwritten Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16504)
*Nicholas Pather,Joshua Fouché,Sitwala Mundia,Karl-Günter Technau,Thokozile Malaba,Alex Welte,Ushma Mehta,Bruce A. Bassett*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 评估17个前沿多模态大语言模型在复杂医疗表格数字化任务中的表现，发现最新模型准确率达85%，加权F1约90%，提示优化可显著提升宏观指标60%以上


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工数字化结构化手写文档成本高、效率低，特别是在中低收入国家，需要自动化解决方案来处理混合打印文本、手写内容、日期和结构化字段的复杂医疗表格

Method: 对17个领先的前沿多模态大语言模型和开源模型进行基准测试，使用包含日期、结构化打印文本、手写回答和显著变异性的真实世界医疗表格，评估模型在离散字段和自由文本提取方面的性能

Result: 较小或较旧模型表现不佳，但最新的Google和OpenAI模型准确率约85%，加权F1约90%。GPT 5.4在噪声日期提取和可靠性方面表现最佳（幻觉率6%），Claude Sonnet 4.6在格式化字段（日期和数值）平均性能最好，Gemini 3.1整体表现最优（WER=0.50，CER=0.31）。提示优化使宏观精度、召回率和F1提升超过60%，但对加权指标改善有限（仅2-5%）

Conclusion: 多模态大语言模型的快速改进为复杂手写工作流程的完全自动化数字化提供了可行路径，这对中低收入国家尤其相关，最新模型已能处理极具挑战性的现实世界医疗表格

Abstract: Manual digitisation of structured handwritten documents is slow and costly. We benchmark 17 leading frontier multi-modal large language models and open-source models against a very challenging real-world medical form that mixes dates; structured, printed text; hand-written responses and significant variability challenges. None of the smaller or older models perform well but the latest Google and OpenAI models reach accuracies around $85\%$ with weighted F1 scores $\simeq 90\%$ across the discrete or predefined fields despite the very challenging nature of the responses. Clear task specific strengths emerge: GPT 5.4 excels in noisy date extraction as well as reliability with the lowest hallucination rate ($6\%$). Claude Sonnet 4.6 had the best average performance across formatted fields (dates and numerical values), while Gemini 3.1 delivered the best overall performance, with the lowest free text error rates (WER = $0.50$ and CER = $0.31$) and the strongest results across discrete classification metrics. We further show that prompt optimisation dramatically improves macro precision, recall and F1 by over $60\%$, but has little impact on weighted metrics (only $\sim2-5\%$ improvement). These results provide evidence that the rapid improvements of multimodal large language models offer a compelling pathway toward fully automated digitisation of complex handwritten workflows that is particularly relevant in low- and middle-income countries.

</details>


### [10] [Predicting Blastocyst Formation in IVF: Integrating DINOv2 and Attention-Based LSTM on Time-Lapse Embryo Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16505)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Niclas Wölner-Hanssen,Reza Khoshkangini,Thomas Ebner,Magnus Johnsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结合DINOv2视觉模型与增强LSTM的混合模型，利用有限每日胚胎图像预测囊胚形成，在704个胚胎视频上达到96.4%准确率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体外受精中胚胎选择依赖人工检查大量延时成像数据，但许多诊所缺乏完整延时系统，且每日图像有限，难以预测囊胚形成

Method: 提出新颖混合模型：DINOv2提取胚胎图像特征，增强LSTM（带多头注意力层）分析时序发育模式，生成最终预测

Result: 在704个胚胎视频数据集上达到96.4%准确率，优于现有方法，且在缺失帧情况下表现良好

Conclusion: 该模型可帮助胚胎学家更高效、更自信地选择优质胚胎，特别适用于成像系统有限的IVF实验室

Abstract: The selection of the optimal embryo for transfer is a critical yet challenging step in in vitro fertilization (IVF), primarily due to its reliance on the manual inspection of extensive time-lapse imaging data. A key obstacle in this process is predicting blastocyst formation from the limited number of daily images available. Many clinics also lack complete time-lapse systems, so full videos are often unavailable. In this study, we aimed to predict which embryos will develop into blastocysts using limited daily images from time-lapse recordings. We propose a novel hybrid model that combines DINOv2, a transformer-based vision model, with an enhanced long short-term memory (LSTM) network featuring a multi-head attention layer. DINOv2 extracts meaningful features from embryo images, and the LSTM model then uses these features to analyze embryo development over time and generate final predictions. We tested our model on a real dataset of 704 embryo videos. The model achieved 96.4% accuracy, surpassing existing methods. It also performs well with missing frames, making it valuable for many IVF laboratories with limited imaging systems. Our approach can assist embryologists in selecting better embryos more efficiently and with greater confidence.

</details>


### [11] [Medical thinking with multiple images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16506)
*Zonghai Yao,Benlu Wang,Yifan Zhang,Junda Wang,Iris Xia,Zhipeng Tang,Shuo Han,Feiyun Ouyang,Zhichao Yang,Arman Cohan,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedThinkVQA是一个专家标注的多图像医学VQA基准，要求模型整合跨视图证据进行诊断推理，现有大语言模型在该任务上表现不佳（最佳模型仅57.2%准确率），主要瓶颈在于跨图像的基础视觉定位和证据整合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学问答基准大多基于单图像分析，而真实临床诊断需要整合多个医学影像视图的证据。当前大语言模型在多图像推理方面存在明显不足，需要专门评估模型跨视图整合证据的能力。

Method: 构建了MedThinkVQA数据集，包含8,067个病例（720个测试用例），平均每个病例6.62张图像，远高于先前工作的1.43张。数据集提供中间监督和步骤级评估，要求模型：1) 解释每张图像；2) 整合跨视图证据；3) 回答诊断问题。通过分析模型性能、错误类型和计算扩展效应来识别瓶颈。

Result: 最佳闭源模型（Claude-4.6-Opus、Gemini-3-Pro、GPT-5.2-xhigh）准确率仅57.2%、55.3%、54.9%；开源模型表现更差。超过70%的错误源于图像读取和跨视图整合。提供专家单图像线索和跨图像摘要能提升性能，而自生成中间结果会降低准确率。推理时间计算仅在视觉定位可靠时有效，否则会放大错误。

Conclusion: 多图像医学推理的主要挑战不是推理长度，而是跨真实世界多模态临床输入的可靠视觉定位、对齐和证据整合机制。模型需要更强的跨视图证据提取、对齐和组合能力，而非单纯增加推理步骤。

Abstract: Large language models perform well on many medical QA benchmarks, but real clinical reasoning often requires integrating evidence across multiple images rather than interpreting a single view. We introduce MedThinkVQA, an expert-annotated benchmark for thinking with multiple images, where models must interpret each image, combine cross-view evidence, and answer diagnostic questions with intermediate supervision and step-level evaluation. The dataset contains 8,067 cases, including 720 test cases, with an average of 6.62 images per case, substantially denser than prior work, whose expert-level benchmarks use at most 1.43 images per case. On the test set, the best closed-source models, Claude-4.6-Opus, Gemini-3-Pro, and GPT-5.2-xhigh, reach only 57.2%, 55.3%, and 54.9% accuracy, while GPT-5-mini and GPT-5-nano reach 39.7% and 30.8%. Strong open-source models lag behind, led by Qwen3.5-397B-A17B at 52.2% and Qwen3.5-27B at 50.6%. Further analysis identifies grounded multi-image reasoning as the main bottleneck: models often fail to extract, align, and compose evidence across views before higher-level inference can help. Providing expert single-image cues and cross-image summaries improves performance, whereas replacing them with self-generated intermediates reduces accuracy. Step-level analysis shows that over 70% of errors arise from image reading and cross-view integration. Scaling results further show that additional inference-time computation helps only when visual grounding is already reliable; when early evidence extraction is weak, longer reasoning yields limited or unstable gains and can amplify misread cues. These results suggest that the key challenge is not reasoning length alone, but reliable mechanisms for grounding, aligning, and composing distributed evidence across real-world multimodal clinical inputs.

</details>


### [12] [SynthPID: P&ID digitization from Topology-Preserving Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16513)
*Suraj Prasad,Pinak Mahapatra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SynthPID：通过从真实图纸中提取管道拓扑结构生成的合成P&ID数据集，显著提升了管道边缘检测性能，仅使用合成数据训练即可达到接近真实数据训练的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: P&ID数字化自动化具有重要价值，但面临数据瓶颈：工程图纸是专有的，公开基准仅有12张标注图像。现有合成增强方法因模板生成器随机散布符号，产生的图与真实工厂不符，导致边缘检测准确率仅约33%。

Method: 提出SynthPID语料库，包含665张合成P&ID，其管道拓扑直接源自真实图纸种子。采用适用于高分辨率图的基于补丁的Relationformer模型进行训练。

Result: 仅使用SynthPID训练的模型在PID2Graph OPEN100上达到63.8±3.1%的边缘mAP，与真实数据训练的oracle模型差距缩小到8个百分点。相比模板生成方法有显著提升，确认生成质量而非模型选择驱动性能。缩放研究表明超过约400张合成图像后增益趋于平缓。

Conclusion: 合成数据生成的结构保真度（而非视觉外观）是提升P&ID数字化性能的关键。从真实图纸中提取拓扑结构作为种子能产生更真实的合成数据，显著缩小合成与真实数据训练之间的性能差距。

Abstract: Automating the digitization of Piping and Instrumentation Diagrams (P&IDs) into structured process graphs would unlock significant value in plant operations, yet progress is bottlenecked by a fundamental data problem: engineering drawings are proprietary, and the entire community shares a single public benchmark of just 12 annotated images. Prior attempts at synthetic augmentation have fallen short because template-based generators scatter symbols at random, producing graphs that bear little resemblance to real process plants and, accordingly, yield only approximately 33% edge detection accuracy under synth-only training. We argue the failure is structural rather than visual and address it by introducing SynthPID, a corpus of 665 synthetic P&IDs whose pipe topology is seeded directly from real drawings. Paired with a patch-based Relationformer adapted for high-resolution diagrams, a model trained on SynthPID alone achieves 63.8 +/- 3.1% edge mAP on PID2Graph OPEN100 without seeing a single real P&ID during training, closing within 8 pp of the real-data oracle. These gains hold up under a controlled comparison against the template-based regime, confirming that generation quality drives performance rather than model choice. A scaling study reveals that gains flatten beyond roughly 400 synthetic images, pointing to seed diversity as the binding constraint.

</details>


### [13] [BARD: Bridging AutoRegressive and Diffusion Vision-Language Models Via Highly Efficient Progressive Block Merging and Stage-Wise Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16514)
*Baoyou Chen,Hanchen Xia,Peng Tu,Haojun Shi,Shan Mu,Weihao Yuan,Siyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BARD框架将预训练的自回归视觉语言模型转换为相同架构的解码高效扩散视觉语言模型，通过渐进式块合并和阶段内蒸馏实现性能恢复，达到3倍解码吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视觉语言模型存在token-by-token解码的推理瓶颈，而直接转换为大块扩散模型会导致性能显著下降，需要一种有效的转换框架来保持性能同时提高解码效率。

Method: 提出BARD框架：1）渐进式监督块合并逐步增大解码块大小；2）阶段内扩散模型蒸馏从小块扩散锚点恢复大块性能；3）混合噪声调度器提高鲁棒性；4）内存友好训练支持长序列处理。

Result: BARD-VL在≤4.4M数据下将Qwen3-VL转换为大块扩散模型，在4B和8B规模上达到同类开放扩散模型的新SOTA，同时相比源模型实现高达3倍的解码吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: BARD框架成功将自回归视觉语言模型转换为解码高效的扩散模型，关键发现是扩散域内蒸馏比直接自回归到扩散蒸馏更有效，为视觉语言模型的高效推理提供了新途径。

Abstract: Autoregressive vision-language models (VLMs) deliver strong multimodal capability, but their token-by-token decoding imposes a fundamental inference bottleneck. Diffusion VLMs offer a more parallel decoding paradigm, yet directly converting a pretrained autoregressive VLM into a large-block diffusion VLM (dVLM) often leads to substantial quality degradation. In this work, we present BARD, a simple and effective bridging framework that converts a pretrained autoregressive VLM into a same-architecture, decoding-efficient dVLM. Our approach combines progressive supervised block merging, which gradually enlarges the decoding block size, with stage-wise intra-dVLM distillation from a fixed small-block diffusion anchor to recover performance lost at larger blocks. We further incorporate a mixed noise scheduler to improve robustness and token revision during denoising, and memory-friendly training to enable efficient training on long multimodal sequences. A key empirical finding is that direct autoregressive-to-diffusion distillation is poorly aligned and can even hurt performance, whereas distillation within the diffusion regime is consistently effective. Experimental results show that, with $\leq 4.4M$ data, BARD-VL transfers strong multimodal capability from Qwen3-VL to a large-block dVLM. Remarkably, BARD-VL establishes a new SOTA among comparable-scale open dVLMs on our evaluation suite at both 4B and 8B scales. At the same time, BARD-VL achieves up to \textbf{3$\times$} decoding throughput speedup compared to the source model.

</details>


### [14] [Operationalizing Fairness in Text-to-Image Models: A Survey of Bias, Fairness Audits and Mitigation Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16516)
*Megan Smith,Venkatesh Thirugnana Sambandham,Florian Richter,Laura Crompton,Matthias Uhl,Torsten Schön*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对文本到图像生成模型的公平性研究进行了系统性综述，提出了偏倚类型和公平性概念的分类法，分析了目标公平性与阈值公平性之间的差距，并提出了超越描述性度量的可操作公平性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像生成模型在各行业广泛应用，但经常表现出社会刻板印象。当前研究领域存在概念模糊性，如"偏倚"和"公平性"缺乏明确区分和操作定义，需要系统梳理和澄清。

Method: 对T2I公平性文献进行系统性综述，构建偏倚类型和公平性概念的分类法，分析目标公平性与阈值公平性之间的差距，调查从提示工程到扩散过程操纵的缓解策略。

Result: 提出了T2I公平性研究的分类体系，识别了目标公平性（规范性理想）与阈值公平性（可操作决策标准）之间的差距，总结了现有缓解策略的局限性。

Conclusion: 提出了超越描述性度量的新框架，通过基于目标的严格测试来操作化公平性，为更负责任的生成式AI开发提供了方法。

Abstract: Text-to-Image (T2I) generation models have been widely adopted across various industries, yet are criticized for frequently exhibiting societal stereotypes. While a growing body of research has emerged to evaluate and mitigate these biases, the field at present contends with conceptual ambiguity, for example terms like "bias" and "fairness" are not always clearly distinguished and often lack clear operational definitions. This paper provides a comprehensive systematic review of T2I fairness literature, organizing existing work into a taxonomy of bias types and fairness notions. We critically assess the gap between "target fairness" (normative ideals in T2I outputs) and "threshold fairness" (normative standards with actionable decision rules). Furthermore, we survey the landscape of mitigation strategies, ranging from prompt engineering to diffusion process manipulation. We conclude by proposing a new framework for operationalizing fairness that moves beyond descriptive metrics towards rigorous, target-based testing, offering an approach for more accountable generative AI development.

</details>


### [15] [Fast Online 3D Multi-Camera Multi-Object Tracking and Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16522)
*Linh Van Ma,Tran Thien Dat Nguyen,Moongu Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多单目相机的快速在线3D多目标跟踪与姿态估计方法，仅需2D边界框和姿态检测，无需3D训练数据或计算密集型深度学习模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有3D多目标跟踪方法需要昂贵3D训练数据或计算密集型深度学习模型的问题，开发一种更高效、实用的在线跟踪解决方案

Method: 采用贝叶斯最优多目标跟踪滤波器的高效实现，仅使用公开可用的预训练2D检测模型，支持多相机系统在相机间歇性断开/重连时的鲁棒运行

Result: 算法在保持准确性的同时显著快于最先进方法，在相机间歇性断开/重连场景下表现出鲁棒性能

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种高效、实用的3D多目标跟踪和姿态估计解决方案，无需3D训练数据，计算效率高，适用于实际部署

Abstract: This paper proposes a fast and online method for jointly performing 3D multi-object tracking and pose estimation using multiple monocular cameras. Our algorithm requires only 2D bounding box and pose detections, eliminating the need for costly 3D training data or computationally expensive deep learning models. Our solution is an efficient implementation of a Bayes-optimal multi-object tracking filter, enhancing computational efficiency while maintaining accuracy. We demonstrate that our algorithm is significantly faster than state-of-the-art methods without compromising accuracy, using only publicly available pre-trained 2D detection models. We also illustrate the robust performance of our algorithm in scenarios where multiple cameras are intermittently disconnected or reconnected during operation.

</details>


### [16] [Privacy-Preserving Semantic Segmentation without Key Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16523)
*Mare Hirose,Shoko Imaizumi,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种新颖的隐私保护语义分割方法，支持每个客户端和图像使用独立密钥，在加密图像上进行模型训练和推理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决隐私保护语义分割中需要为每个客户端和图像使用独立密钥的需求，同时减少加密对模型性能的影响

Method: 模型创建者和客户端使用本地生成的密钥加密图像，在加密图像上进行模型训练和推理；为缓解性能下降，在模型训练中也应用图像加密方法

Result: 在Cityscapes数据集上使用基于视觉变换器（SETR）的模型验证了方法的有效性

Conclusion: 该方法能够实现隐私保护的语义分割，支持独立密钥管理，同时保持模型性能

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel privacy-preserving semantic segmentation method that can use independent keys for each client and image. In the proposed method, the model creator and each client encrypt images using locally generated keys, and model training and inference are conducted on the encrypted images. To mitigate performance degradation, an image encryption method is applied to model training in addition to the generation of test images. In experiments, the effectiveness of the proposed method is confirmed on the Cityscapes dataset under the use of a vision transformer-based model, called SETR.

</details>


### [17] [Expert-Annotated Embryo Image Dataset with Natural Language Descriptions for Evidence-Based Patient Communication in IVF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16528)
*Nicklas Neu,Thomas Ebner,Jasmin Primus,Bernhard Schenkenfelder,Raphael Zefferer,Mathias Brunbauer,Florian Kromp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个专家标注的胚胎图像和自然语言形态描述数据集，用于微调视觉-语言基础模型，实现可解释、透明的自动化胚胎评估，支持基于证据的决策和患者沟通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前人工智能在胚胎选择中的应用存在局限性：需要适应定制临床数据、依赖时差培养箱、缺乏可解释性。现代患者对专家决策提出质疑，特别是在治疗不成功时。需要基于证据的决策理由来支持透明决策和尊重患者的沟通。

Method: 创建专家标注的数据集，包含胚胎图像和相应的自然语言形态描述（胚胎细胞周期、发育阶段和形态特征）。利用该数据集微调现代视觉-语言基础模型，使模型能够学习并随时间提高准确性。预测的胚胎描述可用于自动从文献中提取科学证据。

Result: 提出了一个支持基于语言、可解释和透明自动化胚胎评估研究的数据集。该数据集能够使视觉-语言模型学习胚胎形态特征，生成可解释的描述，并自动关联科学文献证据。

Conclusion: 该数据集有潜力显著增强决策过程，改善患者预后，支持基于证据的决策制定和透明的患者沟通，推动可解释人工智能在胚胎选择中的应用。

Abstract: Embryo selection is one of multiple crucial steps in in-vitro fertilization, commonly based on morphological assessment by clinical embryologists. Although artificial intelligence methods have demonstrated their potential to support embryo selection by automated embryo ranking or grading methods, the overall impact of AI-based solutions is still limited. This is mainly due to the required adaptation of automated solutions to custom clinical data, reliance on time lapse incubators and a lack of interpretability to understand AI reasoning. The modern, informed patient is questioning expert decisions, particularly if the treatment is not successful. Thus, evidence-based decision justification in tasks like embryo selection would support transparent decision making and respectful patient communication. To support this aim, we hereby present an expert-annotated dataset consisting of embryo images and corresponding morphological description using natural language. The description contains relevant information on embryonic cell cycle, developmental stage and morphological features. This dataset enables the finetuning of modern foundational vision-language models to learn and improve over time with high accuracy. Predicted embryo descriptions can then be leveraged to automatically extract scientific evidence from literature, facilitating well-informed, evidence-based decision-making and transparent communication with patients. Our proposed dataset supports research in language-based, interpretable, and transparent automated embryo assessment and has the potential to enhance the decision-making process and improve patient outcomes significantly over time.

</details>


### [18] [PoInit-of-View: Poisoning Initialization of Views Transfers Across Multiple 3D Reconstruction Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16540)
*Weijie Wang,Songlong Xing,Zhengyu Zhao,Nicu Sebe,Bruno Lepri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PoInit-of-View方法，通过攻击3D重建流程中的SfM初始化模块，引入跨视图梯度不一致性来破坏关键点检测和特征匹配，实现对多种3D重建系统的可迁移性投毒攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅将3D重建流程作为一个整体进行反向传播攻击，未能揭示特定模块的漏洞。本文发现结构从运动（SfM）初始化作为许多重建系统的几何核心，可被针对性攻击以实现跨系统的可迁移投毒效果。

Method: 提出PoInit-of-View方法，优化对抗性扰动以在对应3D点的投影处故意引入跨视图梯度不一致性。这些不一致性破坏关键点检测和特征匹配，从而腐蚀SfM中的姿态估计和三角测量，最终导致渲染视图质量下降。并提供理论分析连接跨视图不一致性与对应关系崩溃。

Result: 实验结果表明PoInit-of-View在多种3D重建系统和数据集上有效，在黑盒迁移设置下（如3DGS到NeRF），PSNR比单视图基线提高25.1%，SSIM提高16.5%。

Conclusion: 通过针对SfM初始化模块的跨视图梯度不一致性攻击，能够实现对多种3D重建系统的可迁移投毒，揭示了3D重建流程中特定几何模块的新安全漏洞。

Abstract: Poisoning input views of 3D reconstruction systems has been recently studied. However, we identify that existing studies simply backpropagate adversarial gradients through the 3D reconstruction pipeline as a whole, without uncovering the new vulnerability rooted in specific modules of the 3D reconstruction pipeline. In this paper, we argue that the structure-from-motion (SfM) initialization, as the geometric core of many widely used reconstruction systems, can be targeted to achieve transferable poisoning effects across diverse 3D reconstruction systems. To this end, we propose PoInit-of-View, which optimizes adversarial perturbations to intentionally introduce cross-view gradient inconsistencies at projections of corresponding 3D points. These inconsistencies disrupt keypoint detection and feature matching, thereby corrupting pose estimation and triangulation within SfM, eventually resulting in low-quality rendered views. We also provide a theoretical analysis that connects cross-view inconsistency to correspondence collapse. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our PoInit-of-View on diverse 3D reconstruction systems and datasets, surpassing the single-view baseline by 25.1% in PSNR and 16.5% in SSIM in black-box transfer settings, such as 3DGS to NeRF.

</details>


### [19] [BOOKAGENT: Orchestrating Safety-Aware Visual Narratives via Multi-Agent Cognitive Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16541)
*Bo Gao,Chang Liu,Yuyang Miao,Siyuan Ma,Ser-Nam Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BookAgent是一个安全感知的多智能体协作框架，用于生成高质量、安全意识的视觉叙事故事书，通过联合规划、脚本编写、插图和全局修复不一致性来实现端到端的故事书合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型生成模型在多模态生成方面取得进展，但生成插图故事书仍面临挑战：先前工作主要将任务分解为独立阶段，导致整体多模态基础有限；同时，虽然针对纯文本或纯图像生成进行了安全对齐研究，但现有工作很少将儿童特定安全约束整合到叙事规划和序列级多模态验证中。

Method: BookAgent采用安全感知的多智能体协作框架，从用户草稿出发进行端到端故事书合成，包括联合规划、脚本编写、插图和全局修复不一致性。该方法动态校准页面级文本脚本与视觉布局的对齐，并从时间维度校准整体一致性，通过验证-修正机制处理角色身份和叙事逻辑的全局不一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明，BookAgent在叙事连贯性、视觉一致性和安全合规性方面显著优于现有方法，为复杂多模态创作中的可靠智能体提供了稳健范式。

Conclusion: BookAgent为解决插图故事书生成中的多模态基础和安全约束问题提供了一个有效的框架，通过多智能体协作和全局一致性验证机制实现了高质量、安全意识的视觉叙事生成。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Generative Models (LGMs) have revolutionized multi-modal generation. However, generating illustrated storybooks remains an open challenge, where prior works mainly decompose this task into separate stages, and thus, holistic multi-modal grounding remains limited. Besides, while safety alignment is studied for text- or image-only generation, existing works rarely integrate child-specific safety constraints into narrative planning and sequence-level multi-modal verification. To address these limitations, we propose BookAgent, a safety-aware multi-agent collaboration framework designed for high-quality, safety-aware visual narratives. Different from prior story visualization models that assume a fixed storyline sequence, BookAgent targets end-to-end storybook synthesis from a user draft by jointly planning, scripting, illustrating, and globally repairing inconsistencies. To ensure precise multi-modal grounding, BookAgent dynamically calibrates page-level alignment between textual scripts and visual layouts. Furthermore, BookAgent calibrates holistic consistency from the temporal dimension, by verifying-then-rectifying global inconsistencies in character identity and storytelling logic. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BookAgent significantly outperforms current methods in narrative coherence, visual consistency, and safety compliance, offering a robust paradigm for reliable agents in complex multi-modal creation. The implementation will be publicly released at https://github.com/bogao-code/BookAgent/tree/main.

</details>


### [20] [A B-Spline Function Based 3D Point Cloud Unwrapping Scheme for 3D Fingerprint Recognition and Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16546)
*Mohammad Mogharen Askarin,Jiankun Hu,Min Wang,Xuefei Yin,Xiuping Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于B样条曲线拟合的3D指纹展开方法，将3D点云转换为灰度图像，再利用传统2D指纹识别技术进行识别，显著提升了3D指纹识别的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D指纹识别相比传统2D系统具有接触式、更卫生安全、包含深度和形状信息等优势，但面临拓扑高度变化导致脊谷模式提取困难、配准问题限制采集过程等挑战。

Method: 使用B样条曲线拟合展开3D指纹点云以缓解高度变化和配准限制，将展开后的点云通过相对高度映射转换为灰度图像，然后采用传统2D指纹识别方法进行识别。

Result: 在三个实验中分别获得0.2072%、0.26%和0.22%的等错误率，优于现有方法；在包含不同配准指纹的跨会话实验中达到1.50%的EER，超越了3D指纹展平技术。

Conclusion: 提出的3D指纹展开方法有效解决了高度变化和配准问题，通过转换为2D灰度图像利用成熟识别技术，显著提升了3D指纹识别性能，为实际应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) fingerprint recognition and identification offer several advantages over traditional two-dimensional (2D) recognition systems. The contactless nature of 3D fingerprints enhances hygiene and security, reducing the risk of contamination and spoofing. In addition to surface ridge and valley patterns, 3D fingerprints capture depth, curvature, and shape information, enabling the development of more precise and robust authentication systems. Despite recent advancements, significant challenges remain. The topological height of fingerprint pixels complicates the extraction of ridge and valley patterns. Furthermore, registration issues limit the acquisition process, requiring consistent direction and orientation across all samples. To address these challenges, this paper introduces a method that unwraps 3D fingerprints, represented as 3D point clouds, using B-spline curve fitting to mitigate height variation and reduce registration limitations. The unwrapped point cloud is then converted into a grayscale image by mapping the relative heights of the points. This grayscale image is subsequently used for recognition through conventional 2D fingerprint identification methods. The proposed approach demonstrated superior performance in 3D fingerprint recognition, achieving Equal Error Rates (EERs) of 0.2072%, 0.26%, and 0.22% across three experiments, outperforming existing methods. Additionally, the method surpassed 3D fingerprint flattening technique in both recognition and identification during cross-session experiments, achieving an EER of 1.50% when fingerprints with varying registrations were included.

</details>


### [21] [Co-generation of Layout and Shape from Text via Autoregressive 3D Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16552)
*Zhenggang Tang,Yuehao Wang,Yuchen Fan,Jun-Kun Chen,Yu-Ying Yeh,Kihyuk Sohn,Zhangyang Wang,Qixing Huang,Alexander Schwing,Rakesh Ranjan,Dilin Wang,Zhicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3D-ARD+模型，通过序列化文本到场景生成范式，统一自回归生成与扩散生成，实现复杂室内场景的文本驱动创建


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到场景生成方法要么只生成场景布局，要么只生成物体，且生成的场景布局简单，与包含物体形状、外观和空间排列等非平凡描述的文本输入不一致

Method: 提出序列化文本到场景生成范式，核心是3D-ARD+模型，统一了多模态token序列的自回归生成和下一个物体3D潜在表示的扩散生成。采用两阶段生成：第一阶段在场景空间生成粗粒度3D潜在表示，第二阶段在物体空间生成细粒度3D潜在表示

Result: 构建了包含23万室内场景与配对文本指令的大型数据集，评估了70亿参数的3D-ARD+模型，展示其能够按照文本指令中规定的非平凡空间布局和语义生成并放置物体

Conclusion: 提出了一种新的序列化文本到场景生成方法，通过3D-ARD+模型实现了复杂室内场景的文本驱动创建，解决了现有方法在场景布局复杂性和与文本一致性方面的不足

Abstract: Recent text-to-scene generation approaches largely reduced the manual efforts required to create 3D scenes. However, their focus is either to generate a scene layout or to generate objects, and few generate both. The generated scene layout is often simple even with LLM's help. Moreover, the generated scene is often inconsistent with the text input that contains non-trivial descriptions of the shape, appearance, and spatial arrangement of the objects. We present a new paradigm of sequential text-to-scene generation and propose a novel generative model for interactive scene creation. At the core is a 3D Autoregressive Diffusion model 3D-ARD+, which unifies the autoregressive generation over a multimodal token sequence and diffusion generation of next-object 3D latents. To generate the next object, the model uses one autoregressive step to generate the coarse-grained 3D latents in the scene space, conditioned on both the current seen text instructions and already synthesized 3D scene. It then uses a second step to generate the 3D latents in the smaller object space, which can be decoded into fine-grained object geometry and appearance. We curate a large dataset of 230K indoor scenes with paired text instructions for training. We evaluate 7B 3D-ARD+, on challenging scenes, and showcase the model can generate and place objects following non-trivial spatial layout and semantics prescribed by the text instructions.

</details>


### [22] [See Through the Noise: Improving Domain Generalization in Gaze Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16562)
*Yanming Peng,Shijing Wang,Yaping Huang,Yi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文首次全面研究了标签噪声对视线估计泛化性能的负面影响，并提出SeeTN框架通过语义嵌入空间构建和亲和力一致性度量来缓解标签噪声，提升跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可泛化视线估计方法往往忽略标签噪声对模型泛化性能的影响，而精确的视线标注获取困难导致标签噪声问题普遍存在，需要专门研究其对泛化的负面影响并提出解决方案。

Method: 提出See-Through-Noise框架：1）通过原型变换构建语义嵌入空间，保持视线特征与连续标签间一致的拓扑结构；2）测量特征-标签亲和力一致性来区分噪声样本与干净样本；3）在语义流形中引入亲和力正则化，将视线相关信息从干净样本转移到噪声样本。

Result: 大量实验表明，SeeTN能有效缓解源域噪声的负面影响，在不损害源域准确性的前提下实现优异的跨域泛化性能，凸显了在泛化视线估计中显式处理噪声的重要性。

Conclusion: 该研究首次全面分析了标签噪声对视线估计泛化的负面影响，提出的SeeTN框架通过语义结构对齐和领域不变视线关系增强，显著提升了模型对标签噪声的鲁棒性，为泛化视线估计提供了新的噪声缓解视角。

Abstract: Generalizable gaze estimation methods have garnered increasing attention due to their critical importance in real-world applications and have achieved significant progress. However, they often overlook the effect of label noise, arising from the inherent difficulty of acquiring precise gaze annotations, on model generalization performance. In this paper, we are the first to comprehensively investigate the negative effects of label noise on generalization in gaze estimation. Further, we propose a novel solution, called See-Through-Noise (SeeTN) framework, which improves generalization from a novel perspective of mitigating label noise. Specifically, we propose to construct a semantic embedding space via a prototype-based transformation to preserve a consistent topological structure between gaze features and continuous labels. We then measure feature-label affinity consistency to distinguish noisy from clean samples, and introduce a novel affinity regularization in the semantic manifold to transfer gaze-related information from clean to noisy samples. Our proposed SeeTN promotes semantic structure alignment and enforces domain-invariant gaze relationships, thereby enhancing robustness against label noise. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our SeeTN effectively mitigates the adverse impact of source-domain noise, leading to superior cross-domain generalization without compromising the source-domain accuracy, and highlight the importance of explicitly handling noise in generalized gaze estimation.

</details>


### [23] [Camo-M3FD: A New Benchmark Dataset for Cross-Spectral Camouflaged Pedestrian Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16582)
*Henry O. Velesaca,Andrea Mero,Guillermo A. Castillo,Angel D. Sappa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Camo-M3FD基准数据集，用于跨光谱伪装行人检测，包含配准的可见光-热红外图像对，通过定量指标筛选高前景-背景相似度的样本，为安全关键检测系统提供基础资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伪装目标检测(COD)基准主要关注生物物种，缺乏针对安全关键的人类检测场景的研究，特别是在行人可能伪装融入环境的情况下。多光谱检测虽能缓解低可见性问题，但伪装行人的检测挑战尚未被充分探索。

Method: 从M3FD数据集衍生出Camo-M3FD基准，包含配准的可见光-热红外图像对。使用定量指标筛选具有高前景-背景相似度的样本，提供高质量像素级掩码，并建立基于最先进COD模型的标准化评估框架。

Result: 实验结果表明，热红外信号提供不可或缺的定位线索，而多光谱融合对于细化结构细节至关重要。该基准为开发鲁棒的安全关键检测系统提供了基础资源。

Conclusion: Camo-M3FD填补了伪装行人检测领域的空白，为自动驾驶、机器人和监控等安全关键应用提供了重要的基准数据集和评估框架，促进更鲁棒的检测系统发展。

Abstract: Pedestrian detection is fundamental to autonomous driving, robotics, and surveillance. Despite progress in deep learning, reliable identification remains challenging due to occlusions, cluttered backgrounds, and degraded visibility. While multispectral detection-combining visible and thermal sensors-mitigates poor visibility, the challenge of camouflaged pedestrians remains largely unexplored. Existing Camouflaged Object Detection (COD) benchmarks focus on biological species, leaving a gap in safety-critical human detection where targets blend into their surroundings. To address this, we introduce Camo-M3FD (derived from the M3FD dataset), a novel benchmark for cross-spectral camouflaged pedestrian detection, consisting of registered visible-thermal image pairs. The dataset is curated using quantitative metrics to ensure high foreground-background similarity. We provide high-quality pixel-level masks and establish a standardized evaluation framework using state-of-the-art COD models. Our results demonstrate that while thermal signals provide indispensable localization cues, multispectral fusion is essential for refining structural details. Camo-M3FD serves as a foundational resource for developing robust and safety-critical detection systems. The dataset is available on GitHub: https://cod-espol.github.io/Camo-M3FD/

</details>


### [24] [Real-Time Visual Attribution Streaming in Thinking Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16587)
*Seil Kang,Woojung Han,Junhyeok Kim,Jinyeong Kim,Youngeun Kim,Seong Jae Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种摊销框架，用于多模态思维模型中的实时视觉归因流式处理，通过轻量级学习从注意力特征直接估计语义区域的因果效应，实现与穷举因果方法相当的忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当多模态思维模型从截图生成代码或从图像解决数学问题时，其长推理轨迹应基于视觉证据。然而验证这种依赖具有挑战性：忠实因果方法需要昂贵的重复反向传播或扰动，而原始注意力图虽能即时访问但缺乏因果有效性。

Method: 引入摊销方法，学习从编码在注意力特征中的丰富信号直接估计语义区域的因果效应。该方法训练模型预测因果影响，避免每次推理都需要昂贵的计算。

Result: 在五个不同基准测试和四个思维模型上，该方法实现了与穷举因果方法相当的忠实度，同时支持视觉归因流式处理，用户可以在模型推理过程中实时观察基础证据。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级学习而非暴力计算，可以在多模态思维模型中实现实时、忠实的归因，为模型推理提供即时、可靠的视觉基础验证。

Abstract: We present an amortized framework for real-time visual attribution streaming in multimodal thinking models. When these models generate code from a screenshot or solve math problems from images, their long reasoning traces should be grounded in visual evidence. However, verifying this reliance is challenging: faithful causal methods require costly repeated backward passes or perturbations, while raw attention maps offer instant access, they lack causal validity. To resolve this, we introduce an amortized approach that learns to estimate the causal effects of semantic regions directly from the rich signals encoded in attention features. Across five diverse benchmarks and four thinking models, our approach achieves faithfulness comparable to exhaustive causal methods while enabling visual attribution streaming, where users observe grounding evidence as the model reasons, not after. Our results demonstrate that real-time, faithful attribution in multimodal thinking models is achievable through lightweight learning, not brute-force computation.

</details>


### [25] [AVRT: Audio-Visual Reasoning Transfer through Single-Modality Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16617)
*Edson Araujo,Saurabhchand Bhati,M. Jehanzeb Mirza,Brian Kingsbury,Samuel Thomas,Rogerio Feris,James R. Glass,Hilde Kuehne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AVRT框架通过单模态教师模型生成高质量音视频推理轨迹，通过监督微调和强化学习两阶段训练，在音视频推理任务上实现SOTA性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理模型在文本领域取得显著进展，但在多模态（特别是音视频）场景中仍面临挑战，主要原因是高质量多模态推理数据稀缺

Method: 1) 使用单模态专家模型分别生成视觉和音频推理轨迹；2) 用LLM合并模型融合轨迹；3) 两阶段训练：先用生成的多模态轨迹进行监督微调冷启动，再用大规模数据进行强化学习

Result: 在7个音视频和音频基准测试中，3B和7B参数模型在可比规模模型中达到SOTA，包括OmniBench、DailyOmni（音视频）和MMAR（音频），跨模态训练也能提升单模态任务性能

Conclusion: AVRT框架成功解决了多模态推理数据稀缺问题，建立了新的多模态推理模型训练流程，证明了跨模态训练的有效性和可迁移性

Abstract: Recent advances in reasoning models have shown remarkable progress in text-based domains, but transferring those capabilities to multimodal settings, e.g., to allow reasoning over audio-visual data, still remains a challenge, in part because of the limited availability of high-quality reasoning data in targeted multimodal combinations. To address this problem, we introduce AVRT, a novel framework that generates high-quality audio-visual reasoning traces from single-modality teacher models. We generate independent vision- and audio-reasoning traces via models specialized to reason over their respective modalities and merge the resulting traces with an LLM merger model. The resulting multimodal traces are used in a supervised fine-tuning (SFT) cold start to adapt the target model to audio-visual reasoning traces first, before training it in a second reinforcement learning stage on larger-scale data. Evaluated on seven audio-visual and audio benchmarks, our 3B and 7B parameter models achieve state-of-the-art results among models of comparable size including OmniBench and DailyOmni for audio-visual and MMAR for audio-only reasoning, showing that cross-modal training also transfers to single-modality tasks and establishing a new training pipeline for multimodal reasoning models.

</details>


### [26] [A Benchmark Study of Segmentation Models and Adaptation Strategies for Landslide Detection from Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16663)
*Md Kowsher,Weiwei Zhan,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了CNN、Transformer和大型预训练基础模型在滑坡检测任务中的表现，并比较了全微调与参数高效微调方法（如LoRA和AdaLoRA）的性能效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑坡检测对于灾害响应和风险评估至关重要，但现代分割架构和微调策略在该问题上的相对有效性尚未得到充分理解。需要系统评估不同模型架构和微调方法在滑坡检测任务中的表现。

Method: 使用全球分布同震滑坡数据集（GDCLD），在一致的训练和评估协议下评估代表性的CNN和Transformer分割模型以及大型预训练基础模型。比较全微调与参数高效微调方法（LoRA和AdaLoRA），分析其性能效率权衡。进一步通过比较验证集和保留测试集性能来分析分布偏移下的泛化能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于Transformer的模型实现了强大的分割性能，而参数高效微调方法可将可训练参数减少高达95%，同时保持与全微调相当的准确度。进一步分析了在分布偏移下的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 该研究为滑坡检测任务提供了系统性的基准测试，表明Transformer模型在该任务上表现优异，参数高效微调方法能显著减少计算成本而不牺牲性能，为实际应用提供了有价值的指导。

Abstract: Landslide detection from high resolution satellite imagery is a critical task for disaster response and risk assessment, yet the relative effectiveness of modern segmentation architectures and finetuning strategies for this problem remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we present a systematic benchmarking study of convolutional neural networks, transformer based segmentation models, and large pre-trained foundation models for landslide detection. Using the Globally Distributed Coseismic Landslide Dataset (GDCLD) dataset, we evaluate representative CNN- and transformer-based segmentation models alongside large pretrained foundation models under consistent training and evaluation protocols. In addition, we compare full fine-tuning with parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods, including LoRA and AdaLoRA, to assess their performance efficiency tradeoffs. Experimental results show that transformer-based models achieve strong segmentation performance, while parameter efficient finetuning reduces trainable parameters by up to 95% with comparable accuracy to full finetuning. We further analyze generalization under distribution shift by comparing validation and held-out test performance.

</details>


### [27] [Appearance-free Action Recognition: Zero-shot Generalization in Humans and a Two-Pathway Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16675)
*Prerana Kumar,Martin A. Giese*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 人类和视觉模型在零样本条件下能够泛化到真实世界动作视频的外观无关变换，但准确率低于自然视频。研究者开发的双通路3D CNN模型结合了RGB（形状）和光流（运动）流，通过相干门控机制模拟格式塔共同命运分组，在泛化到外观无关视频方面优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索人类和视觉模型是否能够在零样本条件下泛化到真实世界动作视频的外观无关变换（即保留运动但缺乏静态形状线索的视频）。现有研究通常包含对外观无关视频的显式训练，而零样本泛化能力尚未被充分了解。

Method: 1) 人类实验：22名参与者使用自然视频（UCF5数据集）训练识别5个动作类别，然后零样本测试两种外观无关变换：密集噪声运动视频（AFD5数据集）和随机点外观无关视频。2) 计算模型：开发双通路3D CNN模型，包含RGB（形状）流和光流（运动）流，并引入受格式塔共同命运分组启发的相干门控机制。

Result: 人类参与者在外观无关视频中的动作识别准确率显著高于随机水平，但低于自然视频。计算模型在两种外观无关数据集上都表现出良好的泛化能力，优于当代视频分类模型，缩小了与人类表现的差距。运动通路对外观无关视频的泛化至关重要，而形状通路则提升自然视频的性能。

Conclusion: 运动表征对于泛化到外观无关视频至关重要，支持使用多流架构来建模基于视频的动作识别。研究强调了基于运动的表征在动作识别中的重要性，并为理解人类动作感知的计算基础提供了新见解。

Abstract: Action recognition is a fundamental ability for social species. Yet, its underlying computations are not well understood. Classical psychophysical studies using simplified stimuli have shown that humans can perceive body motion even under degradation of relevant shape cues. Recent work using real-world action videos and their appearance-free counterparts (that preserve motion but lack static shape cues) included explicit training of humans and models on the appearance-free videos. Whether humans and vision models generalize in a zero-shot manner to appearance-free transformations of real-world action videos is not yet known. To measure this generalization in humans, we conducted a laboratory-based psychophysics experiment. 22 participants were trained to recognize five action categories using naturalistic videos (UCF5 dataset), and tested zero-shot on two types of appearance-free transformations: (i) dense-noise motion videos from an existing dataset (AFD5) and (ii) random-dot appearance-free videos. We find that participants recognize actions in both types of appearance-free videos well above chance, albeit with reduced accuracy compared to naturalistic videos. To model this behavior, we developed a two-pathway 3D CNN-based model combining an RGB (form) stream and an optical flow (motion) stream, including a coherence-gating mechanism inspired by Gestalt common-fate grouping. Our model generalizes to both appearance-free datasets and outperforms contemporary video classification models, narrowing the gap to human performance. We find that the motion pathway is critical for generalization to appearance-free videos, while the form pathway improves performance on naturalistic videos. Our findings highlight the importance of motion-based representations for generalization to appearance-free videos, and support the use of multi-stream architectures to model video-based action recognition.

</details>


### [28] [C-GenReg: Training-Free 3D Point Cloud Registration by Multi-View-Consistent Geometry-to-Image Generation with Probabilistic Modalities Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16680)
*Yuval Haitman,Amit Efraim,Joseph M. Francos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: C-GenReg：一种免训练的3D点云配准框架，通过世界尺度生成先验和视觉基础模型，将几何配准问题转移到图像域，实现零样本跨域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于学习的3D点云配准方法难以跨传感模态、采样差异和环境进行泛化。需要一种能够利用生成先验和视觉基础模型优势的免训练框架来解决跨域配准问题。

Method: 1) 使用世界基础模型从输入几何生成多视角一致的RGB表示；2) 利用预训练的视觉基础模型在生成图像中提取密集对应关系；3) 通过原始深度图将像素对应关系提升回3D；4) 引入"匹配后融合"概率冷融合方案，结合生成RGB分支和原始几何分支的对应后验概率。

Result: 在室内（3DMatch、ScanNet）和室外（Waymo）基准测试中表现出强大的零样本性能和优越的跨域泛化能力。首次在真实室外LiDAR数据（无图像数据）上成功运行的生成式配准框架。

Conclusion: C-GenReg通过生成式转移和概率融合，实现了免训练、即插即用的3D点云配准，有效解决了跨域泛化问题，为无图像数据的室外LiDAR配准提供了新解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce C-GenReg, a training-free framework for 3D point cloud registration that leverages the complementary strengths of world-scale generative priors and registration-oriented Vision Foundation Models (VFMs). Current learning-based 3D point cloud registration methods struggle to generalize across sensing modalities, sampling differences, and environments. Hence, C-GenReg augments the geometric point cloud registration branch by transferring the matching problem into an auxiliary image domain, where VFMs excel, using a World Foundation Model to synthesize multi-view-consistent RGB representations from the input geometry. This generative transfer, preserves spatial coherence across source and target views without any fine-tuning. From these generated views, a VFM pretrained for finding dense correspondences extracts matches. The resulting pixel correspondences are lifted back to 3D via the original depth maps. To further enhance robustness, we introduce a "Match-then-Fuse" probabilistic cold-fusion scheme that combines two independent correspondence posteriors, that of the generated-RGB branch with that of the raw geometric branch. This principled fusion preserves each modality inductive bias and provides calibrated confidence without any additional learning. C-GenReg is zero-shot and plug-and-play: all modules are pretrained and operate without fine-tuning. Extensive experiments on indoor (3DMatch, ScanNet) and outdoor (Waymo) benchmarks demonstrate strong zero-shot performance and superior cross-domain generalization. For the first time, we demonstrate a generative registration framework that operates successfully on real outdoor LiDAR data, where no imagery data is available.

</details>


### [29] [LOD-Net: Locality-Aware 3D Object Detection Using Multi-Scale Transformer Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16696)
*Mustaqeem Khan,Aidana Nurakhmetova,Wail Gueaieb,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种集成多尺度注意力机制（MSA）的3DETR改进模型，通过上采样操作生成高分辨率特征图，提升对小目标和语义相关对象的检测性能，在ScanNetv2数据集上取得了显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云数据中的3D目标检测面临稀疏性和缺乏全局结构的挑战，需要更好地捕捉局部几何特征和全局上下文信息。

Method: 在3DETR架构中集成多尺度注意力机制（MSA），引入上采样操作生成高分辨率特征图，结合层次化特征提取和注意力机制。

Result: 在ScanNetv2数据集上，3DETR + MSA模型相比基线在mAP@25上提升近1%，在mAP@50上提升4.78%；但对轻量级3DETR-m变体改进有限，表明需要为上采样策略适配轻量模型。

Conclusion: 结合层次化特征提取和注意力机制能有效增强3D场景理解，但需要针对不同模型架构调整上采样策略以获得最佳性能。

Abstract: 3D object detection in point cloud data remains a challenging task due to the sparsity and lack of global structure inherent in the input. In this work, we propose a novel Multi-Scale Attention (MSA) mechanism integrated into the 3DETR architecture to better capture both local geometry and global context. Our method introduces an upsampling operation that generates high-resolution feature maps, enabling the network to better detect smaller and semantically related objects. Experiments conducted on the ScanNetv2 dataset demonstrate that our 3DETR + MSA model improves detection performance, achieving a gain of almost 1% in mAP@25 and 4.78% in mAP@50 over the baseline. While applying MSA to the 3DETR-m variant shows limited improvement, our analysis reveals the importance of adapting the upsampling strategy for lightweight models. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining hierarchical feature extraction with attention mechanisms in enhancing 3D scene understanding.

</details>


### [30] [iDocV2: Leveraging Self-Supervision and Open-Set Detection for Improving Pattern Spotting in Historical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16726)
*Jose M. Saavedra,Crhistopher Stears,Marcelo Pizarro,Cristóbal Loyola,Luis Aros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于改进编码器(iDoc)和开放集检测器的新模型，用于数字书籍的图形模式搜索，在保持竞争力的同时将搜索速度提升10倍，并在小尺寸非方形查询上达到新的SOTA精度0.612。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数字书籍的大规模普及，需要通过图形模式搜索文档集合变得至关重要。当前历史文档检索和模式发现策略仍有改进空间：SOTA方法整体精度0.494，小尺寸非方形查询精度仅0.427，且处理时间过长（DocExplore数据集需7秒）。

Method: 提出新模型：1) 基于改进编码器iDoc；2) 采用自监督训练策略；3) 集成开放集检测器加速搜索；4) 利用非极大值抑制减少误报。

Result: 模型在模式发现和文档检索方面达到与SOTA竞争的结果，搜索速度提升10倍。在小尺寸非方形查询上实现新的SOTA性能，精度达到0.612（相比之前0.427显著提升）。

Conclusion: 所提模型有效解决了现有方法在精度和速度上的不足，特别在小尺寸非方形查询场景下表现突出，为大规模数字书籍的图形模式搜索提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Considering the imminent massification of digital books, it has become critical to facilitate searching collections through graphical patterns. Current strategies for document retrieval and pattern spotting in historical documents still need to be improved. State-of-the-art strategies achieve an overall precision of $0.494$ for pattern spotting, where the precision for small non-square queries reaches 0.427. In addition, the processing time is excessive, requiring up to 7 seconds for searching in the DocExplore dataset due to a dense-based strategy used by SOTA models. Therefore, we propose a new model based on a better encoder (iDoc), trained under a self-supervised strategy, and an open-set detector to accelerate searching. Our model achieves competitive results with state-of-the-art pattern spotting and document retrieval, improving speed by 10x. Furthermore, our model reaches a new SOTA performance on the small non-square queries, achieving a new precision of 0.612.Different from the previous version, this leverages non-maximum suppression to reduce false positives.

</details>


### [31] [Agentic Large Language Models for Training-Free Neuro-Radiological Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16729)
*Ayhan Can Erdur,Daniel Scholz,Jiazhen Pan,Benedikt Wiestler,Daniel Rueckert,Jan C. Peeken*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的智能体AI管道，用于自动化脑MRI分析，通过LLM协调外部工具执行复杂放射学工作流，包括预处理、病理分割和体积分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型在通用视觉问答中表现优异，但缺乏原生3D空间推理能力，无法直接分析CT/MRI等容积医学影像。智能体AI提供新解决方案，使LLM能够协调利用外部专业工具，无需内在3D处理能力，但在复杂多步骤放射学工作流中的可行性尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出无需训练的智能体管道，使用多个LLM（GPT-5.1、Gemini 3 Pro、Claude Sonnet 4.5）协调现成的领域特定工具，自主执行端到端工作流，包括预处理（颅骨剥离、配准）、病理分割（胶质瘤、脑膜瘤、转移瘤）和体积分析。比较单智能体模型与多智能体"领域专家"协作架构，并引入基于BraTS数据的基准数据集。

Result: 智能体AI能够通过工具使用解决高度复杂的神经放射学图像分析任务，无需训练或微调。系统成功执行从单扫描分割和体积报告到需要多时间点比较的纵向响应评估等复杂放射学任务。

Conclusion: 智能体AI框架能够有效解决复杂神经放射学图像分析任务，通过LLM协调外部工具实现自动化工作流，无需模型训练。研究为未来智能体系统评估提供了基准数据集，并展示了多智能体协作架构的潜力。

Abstract: State-of-the-art large language models (LLMs) show high performance in general visual question answering. However, a fundamental limitation remains: current architectures lack the native 3D spatial reasoning required for direct analysis of volumetric medical imaging, such as CT or MRI. Emerging agentic AI offers a new solution, eliminating the need for intrinsic 3D processing by enabling LLMs to orchestrate and leverage specialized external tools. Yet, the feasibility of such agentic frameworks in complex, multi-step radiological workflows remains underexplored. In this work, we present a training-free agentic pipeline for automated brain MRI analysis. Validating our methodology on several LLMs (GPT-5.1, Gemini 3 Pro, Claude Sonnet 4.5) with off-the-shelf domain-specific tools, our system autonomously executes complex end-to-end workflows, including preprocessing (skull stripping, registration), pathology segmentation (glioma, meningioma, metastases), and volumetric analysis. We evaluate our framework across increasingly complex radiological tasks, from single-scan segmentation and volumetric reporting to longitudinal response assessment requiring multi-timepoint comparisons. We analyze the impact of architectural design by comparing single-agent models against multi-agent "domain-expert" collaborations. Finally, to support rigorous evaluation of future agentic systems, we introduce and release a benchmark dataset of image-prompt-answer tuples derived from public BraTS data. Our results demonstrate that agentic AI can solve highly neuro-radiological image analysis tasks through tool use without the need for training or fine-tuning.

</details>


### [32] [Active World-Model with 4D-informed Retrieval for Exploration and Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16733)
*Elaheh Vaezpour,Amirhosein Javadi,Tara Javidi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AW4RE是一种面向物理感知的生成世界模型，通过4D信息检索和条件生成，为部分可观测环境中的传感决策提供仿真环境支持


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理感知在动态大环境中面临部分可观测的挑战，传统强化学习在全可观测问题上成功但在POMDP等部分可观测问题上进展有限，真实世界探索成本高昂且仿真到真实存在视角未观测问题

Method: AW4RE结合4D信息检索、动作条件几何支持与时序一致性、条件生成补全，构建传感器原生的替代环境，估计动作条件观测过程

Result: AW4RE在极端视角变化、时间间隔和稀疏几何支持条件下，比几何感知生成基线产生更可靠和一致的预测

Conclusion: AW4RE为部分可观测决策问题提供了有效的传感查询探索环境，解决了物理感知中的观测-决策循环挑战

Abstract: Physical awareness, especially in a large and dynamic environment, is shaped by sensing decisions that determine observability across space, time, and scale, while observations impact the quality of sensing decisions. This loopy information structure makes physical awareness a fundamentally challenging decision problem with partial observations. While in the past decade we have witnessed the unprecedented success of reinforcement learning (RL) in problems with full observability, decision problems with partial observation, such as POMDPs, remain largely open: real-world explorations are excessively costly, while sim-to-real pipeline suffer from unobserved viewpoints. We introduce AW4RE (Active World-model with 4D-informed Retrieval for Exploration), an awareness-centric generative world model that provides a sensor-native surrogate environment for exploring sensing queries. Conditioned on a queried sensing action, AW4RE estimates the action-conditioned observation process. This is done by combining 4D-informed evidence retrieval, action-conditioned geometric support with temporal coherence, and conditional generative completion. Experiments demonstrate that AW4RE produces more grounded and consistent predictions than geometry-aware generative baselines under extreme viewpoint shifts, temporal gaps, and sparse geometric support.

</details>


### [33] [Reducing Peak Memory Usage for Modern Multimodal Large Language Model Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16734)
*Junwan Kim,Hyunkyung Bae*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种在预填充阶段进行结构感知KV缓存压缩的方法，以固定内存预算控制MLLMs推理时的内存增长，显著降低峰值内存使用同时保持生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在处理高分辨率图像和长视频序列时，视觉token数量庞大，KV缓存导致内存消耗成为主要瓶颈。现有方法仅在所有输入处理后才压缩缓存，预填充阶段峰值内存使用过高。

Method: 利用MLLMs固有的结构规律性和表示冗余性，提出顺序输入压缩机制，在预填充过程中执行结构感知的键值缓存压缩，强制实施固定内存预算。

Result: 该方法显著降低了峰值内存使用，同时仅带来最小的性能下降，保持了生成性能，实现了更实用和内存高效的多模态推理。

Conclusion: 通过利用MLLMs的结构规律性，在预填充阶段进行顺序输入压缩，能够有效控制内存增长，为内存受限环境下的多模态推理提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have recently demonstrated strong capabilities in understanding and generating responses from diverse visual inputs, including high-resolution images and long video sequences. As these models scale to richer visual representations, inference increasingly relies on storing large numbers of vision tokens in the key-value (KV) cache, making memory consumption a central bottleneck. Existing methods address this issue by identifying redundancy in vision tokens and compressing the cache, but such compression is typically applied only after all inputs are processed, resulting in high peak memory usage during the prefill stage. In this work, we show that MLLMs exhibit inherent structural regularities and representational redundancy that can be exploited to control memory growth throughout inference. Based on this insight, we propose a sequential input-compression mechanism that enforces a fixed memory budget by performing structure-aware key-value cache compression during the prefill process. This approach substantially reduces peak memory usage while maintaining generative performance with only minimal degradation, enabling more practical and memory-efficient multimodal inference.

</details>


### [34] [Incoherent Deformation, Not Capacity: Diagnosing and Mitigating Overfitting in Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16747)
*Ahmad Droby*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 动态3D高斯泼溅方法在单目视频上训练视角PSNR表现良好但泛化能力差：D-NeRF基准测试中平均训练-测试PSNR差距为6.18 dB。研究发现分裂操作导致80%以上差距，变形一致性正则化可显著改善泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态3D高斯泼溅方法在单目视频上表现出明显的过拟合现象，训练视角PSNR与测试视角PSNR存在显著差距，需要探究其根本原因并提出改进方案。

Method: 通过系统消融实验分析自适应密度控制管道（分裂、克隆、修剪、频率、阈值、调度），发现分裂操作是泛化差距的主要来源。提出弹性能量正则化（EER）对每个高斯的变形场施加局部平滑性惩罚，并评估GAD（损失率感知的致密化阈值）和PTDrop（抖动加权的高斯丢弃）等正则化方法。

Result: 分裂操作导致80%以上的泛化差距；EER将差距减少40.8%同时使高斯云增长85%，平均应变减少99.72%；GAD+EER减少差距48%；加入PTDrop和软增长上限达到57%差距减少。方法在Deformable-3DGS架构和真实单目视频（HyperNeRF场景）上也有效。

Conclusion: 动态3D高斯泼溅中的过拟合主要由变形不一致性驱动，而非参数数量。通过变形一致性正则化可显著改善泛化能力，这一发现对动态神经辐射场方法具有普遍意义。

Abstract: Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting methods achieve strong training-view PSNR on monocular video but generalize poorly: on the D-NeRF benchmark we measure an average train-test PSNR gap of 6.18 dB, rising to 11 dB on individual scenes. We report two findings that together account for most of that gap.
  Finding 1 (the role of splitting). A systematic ablation of the Adaptive Density Control pipeline (split, clone, prune, frequency, threshold, schedule) shows that splitting is responsible for over 80% of the gap: disabling split collapses the cloud from 44K to 3K Gaussians and the gap from 6.18 dB to 1.15 dB. Across all threshold-varying ablations, gap is log-linear in count (r = 0.995, bootstrap 95% CI [0.99, 1.00]), which suggests a capacity-based explanation.
  Finding 2 (the role of deformation coherence). We show that the capacity explanation is incomplete. A local-smoothness penalty on the per-Gaussian deformation field -- Elastic Energy Regularization (EER) -- reduces the gap by 40.8% while growing the cloud by 85%. Measuring per-Gaussian strain directly on trained checkpoints, EER reduces mean strain by 99.72% (median 99.80%) across all 8 scenes; on 8/8 scenes the median Gaussian under EER is less strained than the 1st-percentile (best-behaved) Gaussian under baseline. Alongside EER, we evaluate two further regularizers: GAD, a loss-rate-aware densification threshold, and PTDrop, a jitter-weighted Gaussian dropout. GAD+EER reduces the gap by 48%; adding PTDrop and a soft growth cap reaches 57%. We confirm that coherence generalizes to (a) a different deformation architecture (Deformable-3DGS, +40.6% gap reduction at re-tuned lambda), and (b) real monocular video (4 HyperNeRF scenes, reducing the mean PSNR gap by 14.9% at the same lambda as D-NeRF, with near-zero quality cost). The overfitting in dynamic 3DGS is driven by incoherent deformation, not parameter count.

</details>


### [35] [TriTS: Time Series Forecasting from a Multimodal Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16748)
*Xiang Ao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TriTS：一种新颖的跨模态解耦框架，通过将1D时间序列投影到正交的时间、频率和2D视觉空间，解决了长期时间序列预测中的表示瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期时间序列预测（LTSF）面临重大挑战，因为现实世界信号包含高度纠缠的时间动态，难以从纯1D视角完全捕捉。现有方法存在表示瓶颈，需要打破1D视角的局限性。

Method: 提出TriTS框架：1）引入周期感知重塑策略和视觉曼巴（Vim），将1D时间序列转换为2D视觉表示，以线性计算复杂度建模跨周期依赖；2）设计多分辨率小波混合（MR-WM）模块，在频率域中显式解耦非平稳信号为趋势和噪声分量；3）保留时间域的流式线性分支以保持数值稳定性；4）动态融合这三种互补表示。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，TriTS实现了最先进的性能，从根本上优于现有的基于视觉的预测器，同时大幅减少了参数数量和推理延迟。

Conclusion: TriTS通过跨模态解耦方法有效解决了长期时间序列预测中的表示瓶颈问题，提供了一种高效且性能优越的解决方案，为时间序列分析开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Time series forecasting plays a pivotal role in critical sectors such as finance, energy, transportation, and meteorology. However, Long-term Time Series Forecasting (LTSF) remains a significant challenge because real-world signals contain highly entangled temporal dynamics that are difficult to fully capture from a purely 1D perspective. To break this representation bottleneck, we propose TriTS, a novel cross-modal disentanglement framework that projects 1D time series into orthogonal time, frequency, and 2D-vision spaces.To seamlessly bridge the 1D-to-2D modality gap without the prohibitive $O(N^2)$ computational overhead of Vision Transformers (ViTs), we introduce a Period-Aware Reshaping strategy and incorporate Visual Mamba (Vim). This approach efficiently models cross-period dependencies as global visual textures while maintaining linear computational complexity. Complementing this, we design a Multi-Resolution Wavelet Mixing (MR-WM) module for the frequency modality, which explicitly decouples non-stationary signals into trend and noise components to achieve fine-grained time-frequency localization. Finally, a streaming linear branch is retained in the time domain to anchor numerical stability. By dynamically fusing these three complementary representations, TriTS effectively adapts to diverse data contexts. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that TriTS achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance, fundamentally outperforming existing vision-based forecasters by drastically reducing both parameter count and inference latency.

</details>


### [36] [FairNVT: Improving Fairness via Noise Injection in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16780)
*Qiaoyue Tang,Sepidehsadat Hosseini,Mengyao Zhai,Thibaut Durand,Greg Mori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FairNVT：一种轻量级去偏框架，通过适配器学习任务相关和敏感嵌入，应用校准高斯噪声，结合正交约束和公平正则化，同时改善表示层和预测层公平性并保持任务准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去偏方法通常单独处理表示层公平性和预测层公平性，但作者认为这两者本质相关：在表示层抑制敏感信息可以促进更公平的预测。需要一种轻量级框架同时解决这两个层面的公平性问题。

Method: 1. 使用轻量级适配器学习任务相关嵌入和敏感嵌入；2. 对敏感嵌入应用校准高斯噪声；3. 将处理后的敏感嵌入与任务表示融合；4. 结合正交约束和公平正则化，共同减少学习嵌入中的敏感属性泄漏并鼓励更公平的下游预测。

Result: 在涵盖视觉和语言的三个数据集上，FairNVT降低了敏感属性攻击者准确率，改善了人口统计平等和均衡几率指标，同时保持了高任务性能。该框架与多种预训练Transformer编码器兼容。

Conclusion: FairNVT通过统一处理表示层和预测层公平性，提供了一种有效的轻量级去偏框架，在保持任务准确性的同时显著改善公平性指标，证明了两个层面公平性之间的内在联系。

Abstract: This paper presents FairNVT, a lightweight debiasing framework for pretrained transformer-based encoders that improves both representation and prediction level fairness while preserving task accuracy. Unlike many existing debiasing approaches that address these notions separately, we argue they are inherently connected: suppressing sensitive information at the representation level can facilitate fairer predictions. Our approach learns task-relevant and sensitive embeddings via lightweight adapters, applies calibrated Gaussian noise to the sensitive embedding, and fuses it with the task representation. Together with orthogonality constraints and fairness regularization, these components jointly reduce sensitive-attribute leakage in the learned embeddings and encourage fairer downstream predictions. The framework is compatible with a wide range of pretrained transformer encoders. Across three datasets spanning vision and language, FairNVT reduces sensitive-attribute attacker accuracy, improves demographic-parity and equalized-odds metrics, and maintains high task performance.

</details>


### [37] [Bridging Coarse and Fine Recognition: A Hybrid Approach for Open-Ended Multi-Granularity Object Recognition in Interactive Educational Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16785)
*Hanling Yi,Feng Lin,Mao Luo,Yifan Yang,Xiaotian Yu,Rong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HyMOR是一个混合多粒度开放目标识别框架，通过结合MLLM的开放识别能力和CLIP的细粒度识别能力，实现了跨多个语义粒度的准确目标理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLM在开放目标识别方面表现良好但细粒度识别能力不足，而CLIP模型擅长细粒度识别但缺乏对一般目标类别的广泛覆盖。需要一种能够同时处理开放识别和细粒度识别的统一框架。

Method: 提出HyMOR混合框架，将MLLM用于开放和粗粒度目标识别，CLIP模型专门处理特定领域（如动植物）的细粒度识别。同时引入TBO数据集（20,942张图像，8,816个类别）用于内容丰富的教育场景评估。

Result: HyMOR将细粒度识别与CLIP的差距缩小到0.2%，同时将一般目标识别性能比基线MLLM提高2.5%。在所有评估数据集上平均SBert相似度提升23.2%，显著提升了多模态游戏内容生成和交互学习应用的感知准确性。

Conclusion: HyMOR通过混合设计有效整合了MLLM和CLIP的优势，实现了跨多个语义粒度的准确目标理解，为下游多模态内容生成和交互游戏提供了强大的感知基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have enabled open-ended object recognition, yet they struggle with fine-grained tasks. In contrast, CLIP-style models excel at fine-grained recognition but lack broad coverage of general object categories. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{HyMOR}, a \textbf{Hy}brid \textbf{M}ulti-granularity open-ended \textbf{O}bject \textbf{R}ecognition framework that integrates an MLLM with a CLIP model. In HyMOR, the MLLM performs open-ended and coarse-grained object recognition, while the CLIP model specializes in fine-grained identification of domain-specific objects such as animals and plants. This hybrid design enables accurate object understanding across multiple semantic granularities, serving as a robust perceptual foundation for downstream multi-modal content generation and interactive gameplay. To support evaluation in content-rich and educational scenarios, we introduce TBO (TextBook Objects), a dataset containing 20,942 images annotated with 8,816 object categories extracted from textbooks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HyMOR narrows the fine-grained recognition gap with CLIP to 0.2\% while improving general object recognition by 2.5\% over a baseline MLLM, measured by average Sentence-BERT (SBert) similarity. Overall, HyMOR achieves a 23.2\% improvement in average SBert across all evaluated datasets, highlighting its effectiveness in enabling accurate perception for multi-modal game content generation and interactive learning applications.

</details>


### [38] [Improving Radio Interferometry Imaging by Explicitly Modeling Cross-Domain Consistency in Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16794)
*Kai Cheng,Ruoqi Wang,Qiong Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CDCRec是一种多模态射电干涉数据重建方法，通过显式建模跨域一致性来改善射电天文图像质量，采用分层多任务多阶段框架探索域间交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有射电干涉数据重建方法通常局限于单模态域（脏图像或稀疏可见度），导致丢失互补的跨域上下文信息，无法完整建模相互依赖性和一致性。

Method: 提出CDCRec方法，显式建模跨域一致性，设计分层多任务多阶段框架，增强重建过程中域间交互的探索，采用自监督互补建模策略。

Result: 实验结果表明CDCRec通过增强跨域相关性提取提高了成像性能，在严重依赖从受限源域数据恢复密集信息的干涉域转换任务中优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CDCRec通过多模态跨域一致性建模有效改善了射电干涉数据重建质量，为射电天文成像提供了更完整的信息利用框架。

Abstract: Radio astronomy plays a crucial role in understanding the universe, particularly within the realm of non-thermal astrophysics. Images of celestial objects are derived from the signals (called visibility) measured by radio telescopes. Such imaging results, called dirty images, contain artifacts due to factors such as sparsity and therefore require reconstruction to improve imaging quality. Existing methods typically restrict reconstruction to a unimodal domain, either to the dirty image after imaging or to the sparse visibility prior to imaging. Focusing solely on each unimodal reconstruction results in the loss of complementary in-context information in either the visibility or image domain, leading to an incomplete modeling of mutual dependency and consistency. To address these challenges, we propose CDCRec, a multimodal radio interferometric data reconstruction method that explicitly models cross-domain consistency. We design a hierarchical multi-task and multi-stage framework to enhance the exploration of interplays between domains during reconstruction. Our experimental results demonstrate that CDCRec improves imaging performance through enhanced cross-domain correlation extraction.
