In particular, our self-supervised complementary modeling strategy is better than current methods at interferometric domain translations that rely heavily on recovering dense information from constrained source-domain data.

</details>


### [39] [Generative Semantic Communication via Alternating Dual-Domain Posterior Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16796)
*Shunpu Tang,Qianqian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出交替双域后验采样(ADDPS)方法，通过交替执行潜在域和图像域一致性约束，解决生成式语义通信中感知质量受限的问题，相比现有方法获得更优的感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式语义通信接收器依赖MAP估计，无法保持数据分布，限制了感知质量。当前基于扩散的方法使用单域引导存在局限性：潜在域引导对信道噪声敏感，图像域引导继承解码器偏差。简单结合两域会产生过度自信的伪后验。

Method: 将语义解码建模为贝叶斯逆问题，证明后验采样通过保持数据分布实现最优感知质量。提出交替双域后验采样(ADDPS)，在采样过程中交替执行潜在域和图像域一致性约束，将联合后验采样分解为更简单的子问题，避免梯度冲突同时保留两域互补优势。

Result: 在FFHQ数据集上的实验表明，提出的ADDPS方法相比现有方法实现了更优的感知质量。

Conclusion: 通过将语义解码形式化为贝叶斯逆问题并采用交替双域后验采样策略，成功解决了生成式语义通信中感知质量受限的问题，为高质量无线图像传输提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Generative semantic communication (SemCom) harnesses pretrained generative priors to improve the perceptual quality of wireless image transmission. Existing generative SemCom receivers, however, rely on maximum a posteriori (MAP) estimation, which fundamentally cannot preserve the data distribution and thus limits achievable perceptual quality. Moreover, current diffusion-based approaches using single-domain guidance face significant limitations: latent-domain guidance is sensitive to channel noise, while image-domain guidance inherits decoder bias. Simply combining both domains simultaneously yields an overconfident pseudo-posterior. In this paper, we formulate semantic decoding as a Bayesian inverse problem and prove that posterior sampling achieves optimal perceptual quality by preserving the data distribution. Building on this insight, we propose alternating dual-domain posterior sampling (ADDPS), a diffusion-based SemCom receiver that alternately enforces latent-domain and image-domain consistency during the sampling process. This alternating strategy decomposes joint posterior sampling into simpler subproblems, avoiding gradient conflicts while retaining the complementary strengths of both domains. Experiments on FFHQ demonstrate that the proposed ADDPS achieves superior perceptual quality compared with existing methods.

</details>


### [40] [ScenarioControl: Vision-Language Controllable Vectorized Latent Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17147)
*Lili Gao,Yanbo Xu,William Koch,Samuele Ruffino,Luke Rowe,Behdad Chalaki,Dmitriy Rivkin,Julian Ost,Roger Girgis,Mario Bijelic,Felix Heide*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ScenarioControl是首个用于学习型驾驶场景生成的视觉语言控制机制，能够根据文本提示或输入图像合成多样化、真实的3D场景推演，包括地图、随时间变化的反应性参与者3D框、行人、驾驶基础设施和自车摄像头观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前驾驶场景生成方法缺乏对生成场景的细粒度控制能力，无法根据特定文本描述或视觉输入精确控制道路布局和交通条件。需要一种能够连接多模态控制与稀疏矢量化场景元素的机制，以实现对驾驶场景生成过程的精确控制。

Method: 提出跨全局控制机制，将交叉注意力与轻量级全局上下文分支集成，在矢量化潜在空间中联合表示道路结构和动态参与者。该方法支持从场景中不同参与者的视角生成时间一致的场景推演，并支持驾驶场景的长时程延续。

Result: 实验验证表明，ScenarioControl在控制依从性和保真度方面优于所有测试方法。该方法能够生成多样化的真实3D场景推演，包括地图、3D边界框、行人、基础设施和自车观测，支持多视角场景生成。

Conclusion: ScenarioControl是首个支持视觉语言控制的驾驶场景生成方法，通过跨全局控制机制实现了对道路布局和交通条件的细粒度控制，同时保持场景真实性。该方法为自动驾驶系统的训练和评估提供了强大的场景生成工具。

Abstract: We introduce ScenarioControl, the first vision-language control mechanism for learned driving scenario generation. Given a text prompt or an input image, Scenario-Control synthesizes diverse, realistic 3D scenario rollouts - including map, 3D boxes of reactive actors over time, pedestrians, driving infrastructure, and ego camera observations. The method generates scenes in a vectorized latent space that represents road structure and dynamic agents jointly. To connect multimodal control with sparse vectorized scene elements, we propose a cross-global control mechanism that integrates crossattention with a lightweight global-context branch, enabling fine-grained control over road layout and traffic conditions while preserving realism. The method produces temporally consistent scenario rollouts from the perspectives different actors in the scene, supporting long-horizon continuation of driving scenarios. To facilitate training and evaluation, we release a dataset with text annotations aligned to vectorized map structures. Extensive experiments validate that the control adherence and fidelity of ScenarioControl compare favorable to all tested methods across all experiments. Project webpage: https://light.princeton.edu/ScenarioControl

</details>


### [41] [Frequency-Decomposed INR for NIR-Assisted Low-Light RGB Image Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16800)
*Ligen Shi,Zengyu Pang,Chang Liu,Shuchen Sun,Jun Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于频率解耦隐式神经表示（FDINR）的近红外辅助低光照图像恢复方法，通过多尺度小波变换分解频率分量，利用RGB-NIR跨模态频率相关性实现互补优势，解决传统方法中的颜色失真和伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决低光照条件下可见光图像存在的严重噪声和高频结构退化问题，传统方法在空间域刚性融合常导致颜色失真和伪影，需要更有效的跨模态频率域恢复方法。

Method: 基于RGB-NIR跨模态频率相关性统计先验（低频RGB信号更可靠，高频NIR信号相关性更高），通过多尺度小波变换将图像分解为不同频率分量，构建双分支隐式神经表示框架，设计跨模态差异化频率监督机制：利用低光照RGB指导低频亮度和颜色重建，利用高信噪比NIR信号约束高频纹理细节生成，并引入基于不确定性的自适应加权损失函数自动平衡不同频率任务的贡献。

Result: FD-INR不仅能有效恢复图像亮度一致性和结构细节，得益于其隐式连续表示特性，在任意分辨率重建任务中优于现有方法，显著提升了低光照感知的可靠性。

Conclusion: 提出的频率解耦隐式神经表示方法通过利用RGB-NIR跨模态频率相关性，在频率域实现互补优势，解决了传统空间域融合方法的局限性，为低光照图像恢复提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Addressing the issues of severe noise and high frequency structural degradation in visible images under low-light conditions, this paper proposes a Near Infrared (NIR) aided low light image restoration method based on Frequency Decoupled Implicit Neural Representation (FDINR). Based on the statistical prior of RGB-NIR cross-modal frequency correlations, specifically that low-frequency RGB signals are more reliable, whereas high frequency NIR signals exhibit higher correlation, we explicitly decompose images into distinct frequency components via multi-scale wavelet transforms and construct a dual-branch implicit neural representation framework. Within this framework, we design a cross modal differentiated frequency supervision mechanism, leveraging low light RGB to guide the reconstruction of low frequency luminance and color, and utilizing high-SNR NIR signals to constrain the generation of high frequency texture details, thereby achieving complementary advantages in the frequency domain. Furthermore, an uncertainty-based adaptive weighting loss function is introduced to automatically balance the contributions of different frequency tasks, solving the problems of color distortion and artifacts caused by rigid fusion in the spatial domain common in traditional methods. Experimental results demonstrate that FD-INR not only effectively restores image luminance consistency and structural details but also, benefitting from its implicit continuous representation, outperforms existing methods in arbitrary-resolution reconstruction tasks, significantly enhancing the reliability of low light perception.

</details>


### [42] [Infrastructure-Centric World Models: Bridging Temporal Depth and Spatial Breadth for Roadside Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17651)
*Siyuan Meng,Chengbo Ai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出基础设施中心化世界模型(I-WM)的概念，利用路边固定传感器的时空优势来补充车辆中心化世界模型的不足，通过三阶段架构实现从生成场景理解到协同预测的完整系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶世界模型都采用车辆中心化视角，忽略了基础设施视角的独特优势。路边固定传感器具有鸟瞰视角、多传感器融合和持久观测的特点，在时间深度上优于车辆传感器，能够积累长期行为分布包括罕见的安全关键事件，而车辆传感器则在空间广度上更优。这种时空互补性为基础设施中心化世界模型提供了理论基础。

Method: 提出三阶段基础设施中心化世界模型(I-WM)架构：1) 具有质量感知不确定性传播的生成式场景理解；2) 基于物理信息的预测动力学与多智能体反事实推理；3) 通过潜在空间对齐实现V2X通信的协同世界模型。采用双层架构，以无标注感知作为多模态数据引擎，支持端到端生成式世界模型，并规划从LiDAR到4D雷达、信号相位数据和事件相机的分阶段传感器策略。

Result: 建立了驾驶世界模型范式的分类体系，将I-WM定位在LeCun的JEPA、李飞飞的空间智能和VLA架构的理论框架中。提出了基础设施视觉语言动作模型(I-VLA)作为路边感知、语言指令和交通控制动作的统一框架。基于现有多LiDAR流水线，为每个阶段确定了开源基础，为实现能够理解和预测交通的基础设施提供了技术路径。

Conclusion: 基础设施中心化世界模型填补了自动驾驶世界模型研究的重要空白，利用路边传感器的时空互补优势，通过三阶段渐进式架构和I-VLA统一框架，为实现更安全、更智能的交通系统提供了新的研究方向和技术路线图。

Abstract: World models, generative AI systems that simulate how environments evolve, are transforming autonomous driving, yet all existing approaches adopt an ego-vehicle perspective, leaving the infrastructure viewpoint unexplored. We argue that infrastructure-centric world models offer a fundamentally complementary capability: the bird's-eye, multi-sensor, persistent viewpoint that roadside systems uniquely possess. Central to our thesis is a spatio-temporal complementarity: fixed roadside sensors excel at temporal depth, accumulating long-term behavioral distributions including rare safety-critical events, while vehicle-borne sensors excel at spatial breadth, sampling diverse scenes across large road networks. This paper presents a vision for Infrastructure-centric World Models (I-WM) in three phases: (I) generative scene understanding with quality-aware uncertainty propagation, (II) physics-informed predictive dynamics with multi-agent counterfactual reasoning, and (III) collaborative world models for V2X communication via latent space alignment. We propose a dual-layer architecture, annotation-free perception as a multi-modal data engine feeding end-to-end generative world models, with a phased sensor strategy from LiDAR through 4D radar and signal phase data to event cameras. We establish a taxonomy of driving world model paradigms, position I-WM relative to LeCun's JEPA, Li Fei-Fei's spatial intelligence, and VLA architectures, and introduce Infrastructure VLA (I-VLA) as a novel unification of roadside perception, language commands, and traffic control actions. Our vision builds upon existing multi-LiDAR pipelines and identifies open-source foundations for each phase, providing a path toward infrastructure that understands and anticipates traffic.

</details>


### [43] [Modeling Biomechanical Constraint Violations for Language-Agnostic Lip-Sync Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16808)
*Hao Chen,Junnan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BioLip是一个轻量级唇语伪造检测框架，通过分析唇部时间抖动来识别深度伪造内容，不依赖像素级伪影或音视频对应关系，具有跨语言泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前唇语同步深度伪造检测器依赖像素级伪影或音视频对应关系，这些线索编码的是数据依赖模式而非通用物理规律，导致无法跨语言泛化。作者发现生成模型不强制执行真实口面部发音的生物力学约束，会产生可测量的时间唇部方差增加。

Method: 提出BioLip框架，基于64个口周关键点坐标（通过MediaPipe提取），分析时间唇部抖动信号。该方法不依赖像素级特征，而是关注生物力学约束违反产生的时间方差模式。

Result: 发现生成模型产生的时间唇部抖动在说话者的语言、种族和录制条件下具有经验一致性，这种信号可作为跨语言泛化的深度伪造检测基础。

Conclusion: 生物力学约束违反产生的时间唇部抖动是比像素级伪影更基础的检测原理，BioLip框架通过轻量级关键点分析实现了跨语言泛化的唇语伪造检测。

Abstract: Current lip-sync deepfake detectors rely on pixel-level artifacts or audio-visual correspondence, failing to generalize across languages because these cues encode data-dependent patterns rather than universal physical laws. We identify a more fundamental principle: generative models do not enforce the biomechanical constraints of authentic orofacial articulation, producing measurably elevated temporal lip variance -- a signal we term temporal lip jitter -- that is empirically consistent across the speaker's language, ethnicity, and recording conditions. We instantiate this principle through BioLip, a lightweight framework operating on 64 perioral landmark coordinates extracted by MediaPipe.

</details>


### [44] [Re$^2$MoGen: Open-Vocabulary Motion Generation via LLM Reasoning and Physics-Aware Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17807)
*Jiakun Zheng,Ting Xiao,Shiqin Cao,Xinran Li,Zhe Wang,Chenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Re²MoGen：基于增强LLM推理和RL后训练的开源词汇运动生成框架，通过三阶段方法解决现有T2M模型在训练分布外文本描述上的性能下降问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到运动生成模型在训练数据分布内表现良好，但当运动描述与训练文本差异显著时性能急剧下降。需要解决开放词汇运动生成中的泛化能力问题。

Method: 三阶段框架：1) 使用蒙特卡洛树搜索增强LLM推理能力，基于文本提示生成合理的关键帧；2) 应用人体姿态模型作为先验优化全身姿态，通过动态时间匹配目标监督预训练运动生成器的微调；3) 使用物理感知奖励进行后训练，消除LLM规划运动中的物理不合理性。

Result: 实验表明该框架能够生成语义一致且物理合理的运动，在开放词汇运动生成任务上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: Re²MoGen通过结合增强的LLM推理和RL后训练，有效解决了开放词汇运动生成中的泛化问题，为文本到运动生成提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Text-to-motion (T2M) generation aims to control the behavior of a target character via textual descriptions. Leveraging text-motion paired datasets, existing T2M models have achieved impressive performance in generating high-quality motions within the distribution of their training data. However, their performance deteriorates notably when the motion descriptions differ significantly from the training texts. To address this issue, we propose Re$^2$MoGen, a Reasoning and Refinement open-vocabulary Motion Generation framework that leverages enhanced Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning to generate an initial motion planning and then refine its physical plausibility via reinforcement learning (RL) post-training. Specifically, Re$^2$MoGen consists of three stages: We first employ Monte Carlo tree search to enhance the LLM's reasoning ability in generating reasonable keyframes of the motion based on text prompts, specifying only the root and several key joints' positions to ease the reasoning process. Then, we apply a human pose model as a prior to optimize the full-body poses based on the planned keyframes and use the resulting incomplete motion to supervise fine-tuning a pre-trained motion generator via a dynamic temporal matching objective, enabling spatiotemporal completion. Finally, we use post-training with physics-aware reward to refine motion quality to eliminate physical implausibility in LLM-planned motions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework can generate semantically consistent and physically plausible motions and achieve state-of-the-art performance in open-vocabulary motion generation.

</details>


### [45] [A Comparative Evaluation of Geometric Accuracy in NeRF and Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18205)
*Mikolaj Zielinski,Eryk Vykysaly,Bartlomiej Biesiada,Jan Baturo,Mateusz Capala,Dominik Belter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出神经渲染方法的几何精度评估流程与包含19个多样化场景的基准测试，补充传统视觉指标

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经渲染方法虽然发展迅速，但现有计算机视觉指标主要评估生成图像视觉质量，忽视了表面几何精度。在机器人等应用中，几何精度对抓取和物体操作等任务至关重要

Method: 开发专注于几何精度的神经渲染方法评估流程，构建包含19个多样化场景的基准测试，系统评估重建方法在表面和形状保真度方面的表现

Result: 建立了神经渲染几何精度评估框架，提供了系统评估重建方法表面和形状保真度的工具，补充了传统视觉指标

Conclusion: 提出的评估流程和基准测试能够系统评估神经渲染方法的几何精度，为机器人等需要精确几何的应用提供了重要评估工具

Abstract: Recent advances in neural rendering have introduced numerous 3D scene representations. Although standard computer vision metrics evaluate the visual quality of generated images, they often overlook the fidelity of surface geometry. This limitation is particularly critical in robotics, where accurate geometry is essential for tasks such as grasping and object manipulation. In this paper, we present an evaluation pipeline for neural rendering methods that focuses on geometric accuracy, along with a benchmark comprising 19 diverse scenes. Our approach enables a systematic assessment of reconstruction methods in terms of surface and shape fidelity, complementing traditional visual metrics.

</details>


### [46] [Lorentz Framework for Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16836)
*Zahid Hasan,Masud Ahmed,Nirmalya Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于洛伦兹模型的语义分割框架，解决庞加莱球模型数值不稳定问题，实现稳定优化、不确定性估计和层次化表示


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双曲空间语义分割主要依赖庞加莱球模型，存在数值不稳定、优化困难和计算挑战等问题，需要更稳定高效的解决方案

Method: 提出基于洛伦兹模型的双曲语义分割框架，使用文本嵌入引导层次化像素级表示，无需黎曼优化器，可与现有欧几里得架构集成

Result: 在多个数据集上验证了方法的有效性，实现了不确定性估计、置信度图、边界划分、层次化检索和零样本性能，达到更平坦的最小值

Conclusion: 洛伦兹双曲嵌入为鲁棒且具有不确定性感知的语义分割提供了潜力，解决了庞加莱模型的局限性，实现了稳定高效的优化

Abstract: Semantic segmentation in hyperbolic space enables compact modeling of hierarchical structure while providing inherent uncertainty quantification. Prior approaches predominantly rely on the Poincaré ball model, which suffers from numerical instability, optimization, and computational challenges. We propose a novel, tractable, architecture-agnostic semantic segmentation framework (pixel-wise and mask classification) in the hyperbolic Lorentz model. We employ text embeddings with semantic and visual cues to guide hierarchical pixel-level representations in Lorentz space. This enables stable and efficient optimization without requiring a Riemannian optimizer, and easily integrates with existing Euclidean architectures. Beyond segmentation, our approach yields free uncertainty estimation, confidence map, boundary delineation, hierarchical and text-based retrieval, and zero-shot performance, reaching generalized flatter minima. We introduce a novel uncertainty and confidence indicator in Lorentz cone embeddings. Further, we provide analytical and empirical insights into Lorentz optimization via gradient analysis. Extensive experiments on ADE20K, COCO-Stuff-164k, Pascal-VOC, and Cityscapes, utilizing state-of-the-art per-pixel classification models (DeepLabV3 and SegFormer) and mask classification models (mask2former and maskformer), validate the effectiveness and generality of our approach. Our results demonstrate the potential of hyperbolic Lorentz embeddings for robust and uncertainty-aware semantic segmentation. Code is available at https://github.com/mxahan/Lorentz_semantic_segmentation.

</details>


### [47] [XEmbodied: A Foundation Model with Enhanced Geometric and Physical Cues for Large-Scale Embodied Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18484)
*Kangan Qian,ChuChu Xie,Yang Zhong,Jingrui Pang,Siwen Jiao,Sicong Jiang,Zilin Huang,Yunlong Wang,Kun Jiang,Mengmeng Yang,Hao Ye,Guanghao Zhang,Hangjun Ye,Guang Chen,Long Chen,Diange Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: XEmbodied是一个云侧基础模型，通过结构化3D适配器和高效图像-具身适配器，为视觉语言模型注入内在3D几何感知和物理交互能力，显著提升空间推理、交通语义和具身感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前用于训练视觉-语言-动作模型的云侧流水线依赖于通用视觉语言模型，这些模型由于基于2D图像-文本预训练而缺乏几何推理和领域语义理解，导致与复杂物理环境不匹配。

Method: 提出XEmbodied模型：1）通过结构化3D适配器集成几何表示；2）使用高效图像-具身适配器将物理信号蒸馏为上下文token；3）采用渐进式领域课程学习和强化学习后训练。

Result: 在18个公共基准测试中表现稳健，显著提升了空间推理、交通语义、具身感知能力以及分布外泛化性能，适用于大规模场景挖掘和具身视觉问答任务。

Conclusion: XEmbodied通过赋予视觉语言模型内在的3D几何意识和物理交互能力，解决了当前云侧流水线中几何推理不足的问题，为下一代自主系统提供了更高质量的标注基础。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models drive next-generation autonomous systems, but training them requires scalable, high-quality annotations from complex environments. Current cloud pipelines rely on generic vision-language models (VLMs) that lack geometric reasoning and domain semantics due to their 2D image-text pretraining. To address this mismatch, we propose XEmbodied, a cloud-side foundation model that endows VLMs with intrinsic 3D geometric awareness and interaction with physical cues (e.g., occupancy grids, 3D boxes). Instead of treating geometry as auxiliary input, XEmbodied integrates geometric representations via a structured 3D Adapter and distills physical signals into context tokens using an Efficient Image-Embodied Adapter. Through progressive domain curriculum and reinforcement learning post-training, XEmbodied preserves general capabilities while demonstrating robust performance across 18 public benchmarks. It significantly improves spatial reasoning, traffic semantics, embodied affordance, and out-of-distribution generalization for large-scale scenario mining and embodied VQA.

</details>


### [48] [TowerDataset: A Heterogeneous Benchmark for Transmission Corridor Segmentation with a Global-Local Fusion Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16848)
*Xu Cui,Xinyan Liu,Chen Yang,Zhaobo Qi,Beichen Zang,Weigang Zhang,Antoni B. Chan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TowerDataset传输走廊点云细粒度分割基准和全局-局部融合框架，解决现有数据稀缺、长距离结构建模困难、类别不平衡等问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传输走廊点云分割面临真实数据稀缺、难以同时建模全局走廊结构和局部几何细节、现有数据集类别粗糙且场景短小、缺乏长距离结构依赖建模、严重长尾分布和安全关键组件细微区分困难等问题

Method: 提出TowerDataset基准数据集（661个真实场景，约24.66亿点，22类细粒度分类）和全局-局部融合框架：全局分支采用NoCrop训练和原型对比学习捕获长距离拓扑和上下文依赖；局部分支保留精细几何结构；通过几何验证融合和精化两个分支的预测

Result: 在TowerDataset和两个公共基准上的实验表明，所提基准具有挑战性，框架在真实、复杂、异构的传输走廊场景中表现出鲁棒性

Conclusion: TowerDataset填补了传输走廊点云细粒度分割的基准空白，全局-局部融合框架能有效利用全局关系和局部形状细节识别稀有和易混淆组件，为智能电力线巡检提供可靠基础

Abstract: Fine-grained semantic segmentation of transmission-corridor point clouds is fundamental for intelligent power-line inspection. However, current progress is limited by realistic data scarcity and the difficulty of modeling global corridor structure and local geometric details in long, heterogeneous scenes. Existing public datasets usually provide only a few coarse categories or short cropped scenes which overlook long-range structural dependencies, severe long-tail distributions, and subtle distinctions among safety-critical components. As a result, current methods are difficult to evaluate under realistic inspection settings, and their ability to preserve and integrate complementary global and local cues remains unclear.
  To address the above challenges, we introduce TowerDataset, a heterogeneous benchmark for transmission-corridor segmentation. TowerDataset contains 661 real-world scenes and about 2.466 billion points. It preserves long corridor extents, defines a fine-grained 22-class taxonomy, and provides standardized splits and evaluation protocols.
  In addition, we present a global-local fusion framework which preserves and fuses whole-scene and local-detail information. A whole-scene branch with NoCrop training and prototypical contrastive learning captures long-range topology and contextual dependencies. A block-wise local branch retains fine geometric structures. Both predictions are then fused and refined by geometric validation. This design allows the model to exploit both global relationships and local shape details when recognizing rare and confusing components. Experiments on TowerDataset and two public benchmarks demonstrate the challenge of the proposed benchmark and the robustness of our framework in real, complex, and heterogeneous transmission-corridor scenes. The dataset will be released soon at https://huggingface.co/datasets/tccx18/Towerdataset/tree/main.

</details>

### [49] [BrainMem: Brain-Inspired Evolving Memory for Embodied Agent Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16331)
*Xiaoyu Ma,Lianyu Hu,Wenbing Tang,Zixuan Hu,Zeqin Liao,Zhizhen Wu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BrainMem是一种受人类认知启发的无训练分层记忆系统，为具身智能体提供工作、情景和语义记忆，通过将交互历史转化为结构化知识图谱和符号化指导原则，显著提升长时程复杂任务的执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的具身任务规划器大多是无状态的、反应式的，缺乏持久记忆，导致重复错误且难以处理空间或时间依赖关系。需要一种能够积累经验、避免重复错误并处理复杂依赖的智能记忆系统。

Method: 提出BrainMem（Brain-Inspired Evolving Memory），一种无需训练的分层记忆系统，包含工作记忆、情景记忆和语义记忆。系统持续将交互历史转化为结构化知识图谱和精炼的符号化指导原则，使规划器能够检索、推理并基于过去经验调整行为，无需模型微调或额外训练。

Result: 在四个代表性基准测试（EB-ALFRED、EB-Navigation、EB-Manipulation、EB-Habitat）上的广泛实验表明，BrainMem显著提高了不同模型和难度子集的任务成功率，在长时程和空间复杂任务上提升最为明显。

Conclusion: 演化记忆是通用具身智能的一个有前景且可扩展的机制，BrainMem的即插即用设计可无缝集成到任意多模态LLM中，大大减少对任务特定提示工程的依赖。

Abstract: Embodied task planning requires agents to execute long-horizon, goal-directed actions in complex 3D environments, where success depends on both immediate perception and accumulated experience across tasks. However, most existing LLM-based planners are stateless and reactive, operating without persistent memory and therefore repeating errors and struggling with spatial or temporal dependencies. We propose BrainMem(Brain-Inspired Evolving Memory), a training-free hierarchical memory system that equips embodied agents with working, episodic, and semantic memory inspired by human cognition. BrainMem continuously transforms interaction histories into structured knowledge graphs and distilled symbolic guidelines, enabling planners to retrieve, reason over, and adapt behaviors from past experience without any model fine-tuning or additional training. This plug-and-play design integrates seamlessly with arbitrary multi-modal LLMs and greatly reduces reliance on task-specific prompt engineering. Extensive experiments on four representative benchmarks, including EB-ALFRED, EB-Navigation, EB-Manipulation, and EB-Habitat, demonstrate that BrainMem significantly enhances task success rates across diverse models and difficulty subsets, with the largest gains observed on long-horizon and spatially complex tasks. These results highlight evolving memory as a promising and scalable mechanism for generalizable embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [50] [Interdisciplinary Workshop on Mechanical Intelligence: Summary Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16381)
*Victoria A. Webster-Wood,Nicholas Gravish,Amir Alavi,Andres F Arrieta,Sarah Bergbreiter,Anthony Bloch,Laura Blumenschein,C. Chase Cao,Aja Mia Carter,Paolo Celli,Tony Chen,Margaret Coad,Mark Cutkosky,Michael Dickey,Brian Do,Robert Full,Mahdi Haghshenas-Jaryani,Kaushik Jayaram,Aaron Johnson,Eva Kanso,Emma Lejeune,Chen Li,Suyi Li,Jeffrey Lipton,Rob MacCurdy,Matt McHenry,Jean-Michel Mongeau,Todd Murphey,Mark Plecnik,Jordan Raney,Ryan D. Sochol,Hannah Stuart,Zeynep Temel,Michael Tolley,Barry Trimmer,T. J. Wallin,Kon-Well Wang,Wenzhong Yan,Mark Yim,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 2024年机械智能跨学科研讨会总结报告，探讨了通过材料/生物/机器人系统的结构特征实现智能的新范式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械智能（MI）作为一种新兴智能范式，强调通过机械结构本身的响应性、适应性、记忆和学习能力实现智能，与基于电信号和计算机代码的计算智能形成对比。研讨会旨在探讨这一前沿领域的关键问题和发展方向。

Method: 研讨会采用小组讨论形式，38位学术研究人员和8位NSF项目官员参与，通过4-5人和9-10人的小组进行讨论，围绕机械智能主题问题展开，工作小组将讨论记录输入共享演示文稿，并在最后一天进行成果汇报。

Result: 研讨会成功组织了跨学科讨论，参与者通过小组讨论深入探讨了机械智能的关键问题，形成了共享的讨论记录和最终演示成果，为机械智能领域的发展提供了重要参考。

Conclusion: 机械智能作为一个新兴研究领域，通过结构特征实现智能的范式具有重要科学意义和应用潜力，研讨会为促进该领域的跨学科合作和研究方向提供了平台。

Abstract: This report provides a summary of the outcomes of the Interdisciplinary Workshop on Mechanical Intelligence held in 2024. Mechanical Intelligence (MI) represents the phenomenon that novel structural features of material/biological/robotic systems can encode intelligence through responsiveness, adaptivity, memory, and learning in the mechanical structure itself. This is in contrast to computational intelligence, wherein the intelligence functions occur through electrical signaling and computer code. The two-day workshop was held at NSF headquarters on May 30-31 and included 38 invited academic researcher participants, and 8 program officers from the NSF. The workshop was structured around active small and large group discussions in groups of 4-5 and 9-10 with the goal of addressing topical questions on MI. Working groups entered notes into shared presentation slides for each discussion session and presented their outcomes in a final presentation on the last day. Here we summarize the overall outcomes of the workshop.

</details>


### [51] [RHINO-AR: An Augmented Reality Exhibit for Teaching Mobile Robotics Concepts in Museums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16384)
*Nils Dengler,Tim Graf,Leif Van Holland,Patrick Stotko,Reinhard Klein,Maren Bennewitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RHINO-AR是一个增强现实博物馆展览系统，将历史移动机器人RHINO的虚拟重建置于真实博物馆空间中，通过实时环境网格化和交互式可视化让非专业访客理解机器人导航过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前开发的RHINO-VR虚拟现实系统虽然创造了引人入胜的体验，但存在重要限制：访客与真实展览空间和物理机器人分离。RHINO-AR旨在解决这一"现实差距"，将虚拟机器人直接置于真实博物馆环境中。

Method: 在Magic Leap 2头显上使用Unity实现，结合实时环境网格化与交互式可视化（LiDAR感知、可通行性分析和路径规划），使原本不可见的机器人过程对非专业访客可理解。系统在德国波恩德意志博物馆的原展览环境中部署。

Result: 通过为期两天的博物馆研究（22名参与者）评估了可用性、技术性能、满意度、概念理解和与RHINO-VR的偏好比较。结果显示RHINO-AR受到良好接受，有效传达了关键导航概念，且由于更强的物理基础和增加的真实感，通常比VR展览更受青睐。

Conclusion: RHINO-AR成功解决了虚拟现实系统的现实差距问题，通过增强现实技术将历史机器人重新引入其原始展览环境，为博物馆访客提供了更真实、更具物理基础的交互体验，有效促进了非专业访客对机器人导航概念的理解。

Abstract: We present RHINO-AR, an interactive Augmented Reality (AR) museum exhibit that reintroduces the historical mobile robot RHINO into its original exhibition environment at the Deutsches Museum Bonn. The system builds on our previous work RHINO-VR, which reconstructed the robot and the environment in virtual reality. Although this created an engaging experience, it also revealed an important limitation, because visitors were separated from the real exhibition space and from the physical robot on display. RHINO-AR addresses this reality gap by placing a virtual reconstruction of the robot directly into the real museum space. Implemented on a Magic Leap~2 headset using Unity, our system combines real-time environment meshing with interactive visualizations of LiDAR sensing, traversability, and path planning to make otherwise invisible robotics processes understandable to non-expert visitors. We evaluated RHINO-AR in a two-day museum study with 22 participants, assessing usability, technical performance, satisfaction, conceptual understanding, and preference comparison to RHINO-VR. The results show that RHINO-AR was well received, effectively conveyed key navigation concepts, and generally preferred over the VR exhibit due to its stronger physical grounding and increased realism.

</details>


### [52] [Visual-RRT: Finding Paths toward Visual-Goals via Differentiable Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16388)
*Sebin Lee,Jumin Lee,Taeyeon Kim,Younju Na,Woobin Im,Sung-Eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: vRRT：一种结合可微分机器人渲染梯度利用与RRT采样探索的视觉目标运动规划器，无需精确关节角度目标配置


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RRT规划器需要精确的关节角度目标配置，而实际应用中常通过图像或演示视频等视觉观察提供目标，缺乏精确配置信息

Method: 提出视觉RRT(vRRT)：1) 结合可微分机器人渲染的梯度利用与RRT的采样探索；2) 基于前沿的探索-利用策略，自适应优先处理视觉有前景的搜索区域；3) 惯性梯度树扩展，跨树分支继承优化状态以实现动量一致的梯度利用

Result: 在Franka、UR5e、Fetch等多种机器人上实验表明，vRRT在仿真和真实环境中均能实现有效的视觉目标规划，弥合了基于采样的规划与视觉中心机器人应用之间的差距

Conclusion: vRRT通过统一梯度利用与采样探索，实现了无需精确关节角度配置的视觉目标运动规划，为视觉中心机器人应用提供了有效的解决方案

Abstract: Rapidly-exploring random trees (RRTs) have been widely adopted for robot motion planning due to their robustness and theoretical guarantees. However, existing RRT-based planners require explicit goal configurations specified as numerical joint angles, while many practical applications provide goal specifications through visual observations such as images or demonstration videos where precise goal configurations are unavailable. In this paper, we propose visual-RRT (vRRT), a motion planner that enables visual-goal planning by unifying gradient-based exploitation from differentiable robot rendering with sampling-based exploration from RRTs. We further introduce (i) a frontier-based exploration-exploitation strategy that adaptively prioritizes visually promising search regions, and (ii) inertial gradient tree expansion that inherits optimization states across tree branches for momentum-consistent gradient exploitation. Extensive experiments across various robot manipulators including Franka, UR5e, and Fetch demonstrate that vRRT achieves effective visual-goal planning in both simulated and real-world settings, bridging the gap between sampling-based planning and vision-centric robot applications. Our code is available at https://sgvr.kaist.ac.kr/Visual-RRT.

</details>


### [53] [ICAT: Incident-Case-Grounded Adaptive Testing for Physical-Risk Prediction in Embodied World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16405)
*Zhenglin Lai,Sirui Huang,Yuteng Li,Changxin Huang,Jianqiang Li,Bingzhe Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出ICAT方法，通过构建结构化风险记忆库来评估视频生成世界模型在危险场景预测中的可靠性，发现现有模型经常低估或忽略关键危险线索和严重后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成世界模型越来越多地用作具身规划和策略学习的神经模拟器，但很少评估其预测物理风险和严重后果的能力。现有模型经常淡化或忽略危险动作的关键危险线索和严重后果，这可能在规划和训练过程中诱导不安全偏好。

Method: 提出ICAT方法，基于真实事故报告和安全手册构建结构化风险记忆库，通过检索和组合这些记忆来约束风险案例的生成，包括因果链和严重性标签。

Result: 在基于ICAT的基准测试中，主流世界模型经常遗漏机制和触发条件，错误校准严重性，无法满足安全关键具身部署所需的可靠性要求。

Conclusion: 当前视频生成世界模型在危险场景预测方面存在严重不足，需要更系统的方法来评估和改进其安全可靠性，ICAT为此提供了有效的评估框架。

Abstract: Video-generative world models are increasingly used as neural simulators for embodied planning and policy learning, yet their ability to predict physical risk and severe consequences is rarely evaluated.We find that these models often downplay or omit key danger cues and severe outcomes for hazardous actions, which can induce unsafe preferences during planning and training on imagined rollouts. We propose ICAT, which grounds testing in real incident reports and safety manuals by building structured risk memories and retrieving/composing them to constrain the generation of risk cases with causal chains and severity labels. Experiments on an ICAT-based benchmark show that mainstream world models frequently miss mechanisms and triggering conditions and miscalibrate severity, falling short of the reliability required for safety-critical embodied deployment.

</details>


### [54] [LatentMimic: Terrain-Adaptive Locomotion via Latent Space Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16440)
*Zhiquan Wang,Yunyu Liu,Dipam Patel,Ayush Kumar,Aniket Bera,Bedrich Benes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LatentMimic：一种解耦风格保真度与几何约束的四足机器人运动学习框架，通过最小化潜在空间分布差异实现地形自适应运动控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模仿的运动控制方法面临基本优化权衡：严格遵循动作捕捉参考会惩罚地形适应性所需的几何偏差，而地形中心策略往往损害风格保真度。需要一种既能适应复杂地形又能保持运动风格的方法。

Method: 提出LatentMimic框架：1）通过最小化策略状态-动作分布与学习到的动作捕捉先验之间的边际潜在散度，实现对刚性姿态跟踪目标的条件松弛；2）引入带有动态重放缓冲区的地形适应模块，解决不同地形间的策略分布偏移；3）保持步态拓扑结构，同时允许独立的末端执行器适应不规则地形。

Result: 在四种运动风格和四种地形上验证了方法有效性：LatentMimic实现了有效的地形自适应运动，相比最先进的运动跟踪方法获得了更高的地形穿越成功率，同时保持了高风格保真度。

Conclusion: LatentMimic成功解决了运动控制中的风格保真度与地形适应性之间的权衡问题，通过解耦风格约束与几何约束，为四足机器人提供了既自然多样又能适应复杂地形的运动控制器。

Abstract: Developing natural and diverse locomotion controllers for quadruped robots that can adapt to complex terrains while preserving motion style remains a significant challenge. Existing imitation-based methods face a fundamental optimization trade-off: strict adherence to motion capture (mocap) references penalizes the geometric deviations required for terrain adaptability, whereas terrain-centric policies often compromise stylistic fidelity. We introduce LatentMimic, a novel locomotion learning framework that decouples stylistic fidelity from geometric constraints. By minimizing the marginal latent divergence between the policy's state-action distribution and a learned mocap prior, our approach provides a conditional relaxation of rigid pose-tracking objectives. This formulation preserves gait topology while permitting independent end-effector adaptations for irregular terrains. We further introduce a terrain adaptation module with a dynamic replay buffer to resolve the policy's distribution shifts across different terrains. We validate our method across four locomotion styles and four terrains, demonstrating that LatentMimic enables effective terrain-adaptive locomotion, achieving higher terrain traversal success rates than state-of-the-art motion-tracking methods while maintaining high stylistic fidelity.

</details>


### [55] [Learning-Based Sparsification of Dynamic Graphs in Robotic Exploration Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16509)
*Adithya V. Sastry,Bibek Poudel,Weizi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于Transformer和PPO的强化学习框架，用于在机器人探索过程中动态修剪RRT图，减少96%的图规模，实现更一致的探索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人探索算法依赖图结构进行前沿探索和动态路径规划，但这些图会快速膨胀，积累冗余信息并影响性能。需要一种智能方法来修剪这些动态图。

Method: 使用基于Transformer的框架，通过近端策略优化（PPO）训练强化学习策略，在机器人探索过程中动态修剪快速探索随机树（RRT）图，限制图增长并减少冗余信息积累。

Result: 学习到的策略能将图规模减少高达96%；虽然智能修剪的探索速率低于基线，但具有最低的标准差，在不同环境中产生最一致的探索性能；初步证据表明框架能学习将修剪决策与探索结果关联。

Conclusion: 这是首次证明强化学习可用于机器人探索算法中动态图稀疏化的可行性研究，智能修剪能显著减少图规模并提高探索一致性。

Abstract: Many robotic exploration algorithms rely on graph structures for frontier-based exploration and dynamic path planning. However, these graphs grow rapidly, accumulating redundant information and impacting performance. We present a transformer-based framework trained with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) to prune these graphs during exploration, limiting their growth and reducing the accumulation of excess information. The framework was evaluated on simulations of a robotic agent using Rapidly Exploring Random Trees (RRT) to carry out frontier-based exploration, where the learned policy reduces graph size by up to 96%. We find preliminary evidence that our framework learns to associate pruning decisions with exploration outcomes despite sparse, delayed reward signals. We also observe that while intelligent pruning achieves a lower rate of exploration compared to baselines, it yields the lowest standard deviation, producing the most consistent exploration across varied environments. To the best of our knowledge, these results are the first suggesting the viability of RL in sparsification of dynamic graphs used in robotic exploration algorithms.

</details>


### [56] [Human Cognition in Machines: A Unified Perspective of World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16592)
*Timothy Rupprecht,Pu Zhao,Amir Taherin,Arash Akbari,Arman Akbari,Yumei He,Sean Duffy,Juyi Lin,Yixiao Chen,Rahul Chowdhury,Enfu Nan,Yixin Shen,Yifan Cao,Haochen Zeng,Weiwei Chen,Geng Yuan,Jennifer Dy,Sarah Ostadabbas,Silvia Zhang,David Kaeli,Edmund Yeh,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个基于认知架构理论的统一框架来评估世界模型，识别了动机和元认知的研究空白，并引入了"认知世界模型"新类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究声称世界模型具有"类人"认知能力，但缺乏基于认知架构理论的系统性评估框架。需要建立统一的理论基础来正确评估这些主张，并指导未来研究方向。

Method: 提出基于认知架构理论的概念统一框架，涵盖记忆、感知、语言、推理、想象、动机和元认知等所有认知功能。应用该分类法分析视频、具身和认知世界模型，识别研究空白。

Result: 发现动机（特别是内在动机）和元认知领域研究严重不足。提出了基于主动推理和全局工作空间理论的具体研究方向。引入了"认知世界模型"新类别，涵盖基于结构化知识的科学发现智能体框架。

Conclusion: 该统一框架为评估世界模型的认知能力提供了理论基础，识别了关键研究空白，并提出了具体解决方案。该分类法比现有分类法更能指导未来研究方向，特别是在动机、元认知和科学发现应用方面。

Abstract: This comprehensive report distinguishes prior works by the cognitive functions they innovate. Many works claim an almost "human-like" cognitive capability in their world models. To evaluate these claims requires a proper grounding in first principles in Cognitive Architecture Theory (CAT). We present a conceptual unified framework for world models that fully incorporates all the cognitive functions associated with CAT (i.e. memory, perception, language, reasoning, imagining, motivation, and meta-cognition) and identify gaps in the research as a guide for future states of the art. In particular, we find that motivation (especially intrinsic motivation) and meta-cognition remain drastically under-researched, and we propose concrete directions informed by active inference and global workspace theory to address them. We further introduce Epistemic World Models, a new category encompassing agent frameworks for scientific discovery that operate over structured knowledge. Our taxonomy, applied across video, embodied, and epistemic world models, suggests research directions where prior taxonomies have not.

</details>


### [57] [Emergency Stopping for Liquid-manipulating Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16667)
*Samuli Hynninen,Ville Kyrki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于机器人操纵开放容器中液体的紧急停止系统，通过最优控制生成无溢出的快速停止轨迹


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液体对容器加速度和急动度高度敏感，虽然无溢出液体操纵已被广泛研究，但在意外危险下的紧急停止却很少受到关注，而突然制动可能导致危险溢出

Method: 将紧急停止问题表述为最优控制问题，在模型预测控制框架中求解，生成时间最优、无溢出的停止轨迹；该方法作为即插即用的安全层，可叠加在现有的无晃动运动规划方法之上

Result: 通过仿真和7自由度Franka Emika Panda机器人实验证明，所提方法能够实现快速紧急停止而不发生溢出

Conclusion: 提出的紧急停止系统能够有效应对意外危险，在考虑非线性液体动力学的同时实现快速、无溢出的安全停止

Abstract: Manipulating open liquid containers is challenging because liquids are highly sensitive to vessel accelerations and jerks. Although spill-free liquid manipulation has been widely studied, emergency stopping under unexpected hazards has received little attention, despite the fact that abrupt braking may cause hazardous spills. This letter presents an emergency stop system for robots manipulating liquids in open containers. We formulate emergency stopping as an optimal control problem and solve it in a model predictive control framework to generate time-optimal, spill-free stopping trajectories. The method operates as a plug-and-play safety layer on top of existing slosh-free motion planning methods, enabling immediate reaction to detected hazards while accounting for nonlinear liquid dynamics. We demonstrate, through simulation and on a 7-DoF Franka Emika Panda robot, that the proposed approach achieves fast emergency stopping without spilling.

</details>


### [58] [Diffusion-Based Optimization for Accelerated Convergence of Redundant Dual-Arm Minimum Time Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16670)
*Jushan Chen,Jonathan Fried,Santiago Paternain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于扩散算法的框架，通过概率采样解决双臂机器人笛卡尔路径跟踪的非凸优化问题，相比之前梯度方法实现35倍加速和34%误差降低


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前基于梯度的方法计算开销大，且由于梯度稀疏性无法直接施加笛卡尔误差约束，需要更高效的方法解决双臂机器人路径跟踪的非凸优化问题

Method: 提出基于扩散算法的框架，利用概率采样处理非凸高层问题，替代之前的梯度方法，能够直接施加L∞笛卡尔误差约束

Result: 相比先前工作，运行时间减少35倍，笛卡尔误差降低34%

Conclusion: 扩散算法框架通过概率采样有效解决了双臂机器人路径跟踪的非凸优化问题，显著提升了计算效率和精度

Abstract: We present a framework leveraging a novel variant of the model-based diffusion algorithm to minimize the time required for a redundant dual-arm robot configuration to follow a desired relative Cartesian path. Our prior work proposed a bi-level optimization approach for the dual-arm problem, where we derived the analytical solution to the lower-level convex sub-problem and solved the high-level nonconvex problem using a primal-dual approach. However, the gradient-based nature leads to a large computation overhead, and it prohibits directly imposing an $L_{\infty}$ Cartesian error constraint along the joint trajectory due to the sparsity of the gradient. In this work, we propose a diffusion-based framework that relies on probabilistic sampling to tackle the aforementioned challenges in the nonconvex high-level problem, leading to a 35x reduction in the runtime and 34\% less Cartesian error compared to our prior work.

</details>


### [59] [ReconVLA: An Uncertainty-Guided and Failure-Aware Vision-Language-Action Framework for Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16677)
*Lingling Chen,Zongyao Lyu,William J. Beksi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReconVLA：一种为视觉-语言-动作模型提供校准不确定性估计的可靠保形预测方法，通过动作令牌和状态空间的保形预测实现故障检测和置信度校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型缺乏对其动作预测的校准置信度度量，限制了其在需要预测不确定性和故障的真实世界环境中的可靠性。

Method: 将保形预测直接应用于预训练VLA策略的动作令牌输出，获得校准的不确定性估计；同时将保形预测扩展到机器人状态空间，在故障发生前检测异常或不安全状态。

Result: 在模拟和真实机器人实验中，保形化的动作预测能持续改进故障预测、减少灾难性错误，并提供校准的置信度度量，无需重新训练或修改底层VLA模型。

Conclusion: ReconVLA通过保形预测为VLA模型提供了可靠的不确定性估计和故障检测机制，显著提高了机器人控制系统的可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have emerged as generalist robotic controllers capable of mapping visual observations and natural language instructions to continuous action sequences. However, VLAs provide no calibrated measure of confidence in their action predictions, thus limiting their reliability in real-world settings where uncertainty and failures must be anticipated. To address this problem we introduce ReconVLA, a reliable conformal model that produces uncertainty-guided and failure-aware control signals. Concretely, our approach applies conformal prediction directly to the action token outputs of pretrained VLA policies, yielding calibrated uncertainty estimates that correlate with execution quality and task success. Furthermore, we extend conformal prediction to the robot state space to detect outliers or unsafe states before failures occur, providing a simple yet effective failure detection mechanism that complements the action-level uncertainty. We evaluate ReconVLA in both simulation and real robot experiments across diverse manipulation tasks. Our results show that conformalized action predictions consistently improve failure anticipation, reduce catastrophic errors, and provide a calibrated measure of confidence without retraining or modifying the underlying VLA.

</details>


### [60] [Rewind-IL: Online Failure Detection and State Respawning for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16683)
*Gehan Zheng,Sanjay Seenivasan,Matthew Johnson-Roberson,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Rewind-IL：一种无需训练的在线安全保障框架，用于生成式动作块模仿策略，通过零样本故障检测和状态回滚机制提高长时程操作任务的可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习使机器人能够从演示中学习复杂的视觉运动操作技能，但部署失败仍然是主要障碍，特别是对于长时程动作块策略。一旦执行偏离演示流形，这些策略通常会继续产生局部合理的动作而无法从故障中恢复。现有的运行时监控器要么需要故障数据，要么在良性特征漂移时过度触发，要么只停留在故障检测而不提供恢复机制。

Method: Rewind-IL结合了基于时间跨块差异估计（TIDE）的零样本故障检测器（使用分割共形预测校准）和状态重生机制。离线阶段，视觉语言模型识别演示中的恢复检查点，冻结策略编码器用于构建紧凑的检查点特征数据库。在线阶段，Rewind-IL监控重叠动作块的自一致性，跟踪与检查点库的相似性，并在故障时将执行回滚到最新验证的安全状态，然后从干净的策略状态重新开始推理。

Result: 在真实世界和模拟的长时程操作任务（包括迁移到流匹配动作块策略）上的实验表明，策略内部一致性结合语义基础的重生为模仿学习的可靠性改进提供了实用途径。

Conclusion: Rewind-IL框架通过零样本故障检测和语义验证的状态回滚机制，为生成式动作块模仿策略提供了有效的在线安全保障，显著提高了长时程操作任务的可靠性，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Imitation learning has enabled robots to acquire complex visuomotor manipulation skills from demonstrations, but deployment failures remain a major obstacle, especially for long-horizon action-chunked policies. Once execution drifts off the demonstration manifold, these policies often continue producing locally plausible actions without recovering from the failure. Existing runtime monitors either require failure data, over-trigger under benign feature drift, or stop at failure detection without providing a recovery mechanism. We present Rewind-IL, a training-free online safeguard framework for generative action-chunked imitation policies. Rewind-IL combines a zero-shot failure detector based on Temporal Inter-chunk Discrepancy Estimate (TIDE), calibrated with split conformal prediction, with a state-respawning mechanism that returns the robot to a semantically verified safe intermediate state. Offline, a vision-language model identifies recovery checkpoints in demonstrations, and the frozen policy encoder is used to construct a compact checkpoint feature database. Online, Rewind-IL monitors self-consistency in overlapping action chunks, tracks similarity to the checkpoint library, and, upon failure, rewinds execution to the latest verified safe state before restarting inference from a clean policy state. Experiments on real-world and simulated long-horizon manipulation tasks, including transfer to flow-matching action-chunked policies, demonstrate that policy-internal consistency coupled with semantically grounded respawning offers a practical route to improved reliability in imitation learning. Supplemental materials are available at https://sjay05.github.io/rewind-il

</details>


### [61] [LiDAR-based Crowd Navigation with Visible Edge Group Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16741)
*Allan Wang,Aaron Steinfeld*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于可见边缘的群体表示方法，用于机器人在密集人群中的导航，相比传统方法在保持安全性和社交性的同时提高了计算速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在拥挤行人环境中，基于行人群体的导航已被证明能使机器人行为更安全、更社交。然而，现有方法要么在有限场景中探索群体，要么依赖易受遮挡影响的外部检测模块，这些方法在密集人群中存在噪声和误差问题。

Method: 提出可见边缘基群体表示方法，简化群体预测。通过仿真实验验证导航框架与简化群体表示结合的性能，并在真实机器人上部署以探索实际应用价值。

Result: 研究表明群体预测精度对密集环境中的导航性能影响有限。仿真实验显示，集成简化群体表示的导航框架在密集人群中保持相当的安全性和社交性，同时实现更快的计算速度。真实机器人部署验证了群体基表示的实际效益。

Conclusion: 可见边缘基群体表示为机器人在密集人群中的导航提供了一种实用解决方案，在保持导航质量的同时显著提高了计算效率，适合实际部署。

Abstract: Robot navigation in crowded pedestrian environments is a well-known challenge and we explore the practical deployment of group-based representations in this setting. Pedestrian groups have been empirically shown to enable a mobile robot's navigation behavior to be safer and more social. However, existing approaches either explored groups only in limited scenarios with no high-density crowds or depended on external detection modules to track individuals, which are prone to noise and errors due to occlusions in crowds. We show that group prediction accuracy affects navigation performance only marginally in crowded environments. Based on this observation, we propose the visible edge-based group representation. We additionally demonstrate via simulation experiments that our navigation framework, integrated with the simplified group representation, performs comparatively in terms of safety and socialness in dense crowds, while achieving faster computation speed. Finally, we deploy our navigation framework on a real robot to explore the benefits of practically deploying group-based representations in the real world.

</details>


### [62] [LongBench: Evaluating Robotic Manipulation Policies on Real-World Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16788)
*Xueyao Chen,Jingkai Jia,Tong Yang,Yibo Fu,Wei Li,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LongBench是一个用于评估长时程机器人操作的真实世界基准测试，包含1000多个真实世界片段，涵盖上下文无关（完全可观测）和上下文相关（模糊驱动）两种互补机制，旨在分析长时程操作失败的具体原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略在长时间执行中经常性能下降，但现有基准测试难以揭示失败的具体原因。大多数现有基准要么基于仿真，要么只报告总体成功率，难以区分真实世界执行中不同时间难度的具体来源。

Method: 开发了LongBench基准测试，包含1000多个真实世界片段，分为两种互补机制：上下文无关（完全可观测）和上下文相关（模糊驱动）。通过将任务组织为能力特定和模糊特定子集，实现对执行鲁棒性、时间一致性和上下文相关推理的机制感知评估。

Result: 评估六个最先进策略发现，长时程性能不受单一因素支配。在完全可观测设置中，性能与执行鲁棒性更密切相关，而上下文难度因任务而异，且基于记忆的方法并未一致改善性能。

Conclusion: LongBench可作为研究长时程操作的有用基准，有助于开发在执行和上下文挑战方面具有更强鲁棒性的策略。长时程性能的复杂性表明需要更细致的评估方法来理解不同失败机制。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation policies often degrade over extended horizons, yet existing benchmarks provide limited insight into why such failures occur. Most prior benchmarks are either simulation-based or report aggregate success, making it difficult to disentangle the distinct sources of temporal difficulty in real-world execution. We introduce LongBench, a real-world benchmark for evaluating long-horizon manipulation. LongBench consists of over 1,000 real-world episodes, covering two complementary regimes: Context-Independent (fully observable) and Context-Dependent (ambiguity-driven). By organizing tasks into capability- and ambiguity-specific subsets, LongBench enables mechanism-aware evaluation of execution robustness, temporal consistency, and context-dependent reasoning. Evaluating six state-of-the-art policies reveals that long-horizon performance is not governed by a single factor. We observe that performance in fully observable settings is more strongly associated with execution robustness, while contextual difficulty varies across tasks and is not consistently improved by memory-based methods. We hope that LongBench serves as a useful benchmark for studying long-horizon manipulation and for developing policies with stronger robustness across both execution and contextual challenges.

</details>


### [63] [Refinement of Accelerated Demonstrations via Incremental Iterative Reference Learning Control for Fast Contact-Rich Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16850)
*Koki Yamane,Cristian C. Beltran-Hernandez,Steven Oh,Masashi Hamaya,Sho Sakaino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出I2RLC方法，通过渐进加速和参考轨迹迭代更新，实现接触丰富操作任务的高保真快速演示生成，用于模仿学习


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务需要快速执行，但为模仿学习提供快速演示面临挑战：人类无法高速演示，简单加速演示会改变接触动力学并导致大跟踪误差

Method: 提出增量迭代参考学习控制(I2RLC)，在迭代参考学习控制(IRLC)基础上，通过渐进增加速度同时更新参考轨迹，生成高保真轨迹

Result: IRLC和I2RLC均实现高达10倍加速演示且减少跟踪误差；I2RLC相比IRLC在三个任务和多种速度下平均提高22.5%的空间相似性；使用精炼轨迹训练的模仿学习策略在孔轴装配任务中达到100%成功率

Conclusion: 渐进速度调度结合参考轨迹适应为快速、接触丰富的模仿学习提供了实用路径，I2RLC训练的接触力更低

Abstract: Fast execution of contact-rich manipulation is critical for practical deployment, yet providing fast demonstrations for imitation learning (IL) remains challenging: humans cannot demonstrate at high speed, and naively accelerating demonstrations alters contact dynamics and induces large tracking errors. We present a method to autonomously refine time-accelerated demonstrations by repurposing Iterative Reference Learning Control (IRLC) to iteratively update the reference trajectory from observed tracking errors. However, applying IRLC directly at high speed tends to produce larger early-iteration errors and less stable transients. To address this issue, we propose Incremental Iterative Reference Learning Control (I2RLC), which gradually increases the speed while updating the reference, yielding high-fidelity trajectories. We validate on real-robot whiteboard erasing and peg-in-hole tasks using a teleoperation setup with a compliance-controlled follower and a 3D-printed haptic leader. Both IRLC and I2RLC achieve up to 10x faster demonstrations with reduced tracking error; moreover, I2RLC improves spatial similarity to the original trajectories by 22.5% on average over IRLC across three tasks and multiple speeds (3x-10x). We then use the refined trajectories to train IL policies; the resulting policies execute faster than the demonstrations and achieve 100% success rates in the peg-in-hole task at both seen and unseen positions, with I2RLC-trained policies exhibiting lower contact forces than those trained on IRLC-refined demonstrations. These results indicate that gradual speed scheduling coupled with reference adaptation provides a practical path to fast, contact-rich IL.

</details>


### [64] [Greedy Kalman-Swarm: Improving State Estimation in Robot Swarms in Harsh Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16868)
*Phunyapa Suksomboon,Paulo Garcia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为"Greedy Kalman-Swarm"的分布式状态估计方法，允许机器人仅利用局部邻居数据而非全局通信来提升群体状态估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人群体中，传统状态估计方法依赖集中式处理或大量通信协议来实现全局共识，这在现实部署中通常不切实际。需要一种能够在通信受限环境下保持群体协调性的分布式估计方法。

Method: 采用局部"贪婪"的分布式状态估计方法，个体机器人利用相对机器人间传感数据，在每个迭代中整合所有当前可用的邻居数据来优化内部状态，无需完整数据可用性或全局通信。

Result: 在通信受限环境中的仿真表明，机器人能够有效整合邻居数据提升精度，即使在数据缺失时也能保持鲁棒性和功能性。该方法在独立估计的低开销和集中式系统的高精度之间取得了平衡。

Conclusion: 全局状态感知可以是涌现的而非强制实施的，这为在不可预测地形中保持群体凝聚力提供了可扩展框架。这种去中心化方法为更具韧性的自主系统奠定了基础，特别适用于搜救或太空探索等无法保证可靠高带宽通信的任务。

Abstract: State estimation is a fundamental requirement in robotics, where the accurate determination of a robot's state is essential for stable operation despite inherent process disturbances and sensor noise. Traditionally, this is achieved through Kalman filtering, providing a statistically optimal estimate by balancing predictive models with noisy measurements. In the context of robotic swarms, the challenge shifts from individual accuracy to collective coordination, where the integration of global dynamics can significantly enhance the precision of the entire group. Existing estimation techniques rely on centralized processing or heavy communication protocols to reach a global consensus, which are frequently impractical in real-world deployments. Here we show that a localized, "greedy" approach to distributed state estimation (termed "Greedy Kalman-Swarm") allows individual robots to leverage relative inter-robot sensing for improved accuracy without requiring full data availability or global communication. Simulations in communication-constrained environments show robots can effectively integrate all currently available neighbor data at each iteration to refine their internal states, yet remain robust and functional even when data is missing. This results in a performance profile that strikes a balance between the low overhead of independent estimation and the high accuracy of centralized systems, specifically under harsh or dynamic environmental conditions. Our results demonstrate that global state awareness can be emergent rather than enforced, providing a scalable framework for maintaining swarm cohesion in unpredictable terrains. We anticipate that this decentralized methodology will serve as a foundation for more resilient autonomous systems, particularly in search-and-rescue or space exploration missions where reliable, high-bandwidth communication cannot be guaranteed.

</details>


### [65] [Chain Of Interaction Benchmark (COIN): When Reasoning meets Embodied Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16886)
*Xianhao Wang,Xiaojian Ma,Haozhe Hu,Rongpeng Su,Yutian Cheng,Zhou Ziheng,Hangxin Liu,Lei Liu,Bin Li,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: COIN是一个评估具身智能体交互推理能力的基准测试，包含50个日常交互任务、原始任务数据集和组合任务，通过系统化指标评估现有方法的局限性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试未能系统评估具身智能体在部分可观测环境中的交互推理能力，这种能力对于解决长时程、因果依赖的现实任务至关重要

Method: 构建COIN-50（50个日常交互任务）、COIN-Primitive（因果依赖任务所需原始任务）和COIN-Composition（中等复杂度组合任务）；开发低成本移动AR遥操作系统并收集COIN-Primitive数据集（1000个演示）；设计执行稳定性和泛化鲁棒性评估指标

Result: 对CodeAsPolicy、VLA和语言条件H-VLA方法的评估显示，现有模型在交互推理任务上表现不佳，视觉理解与运动执行之间存在显著差距

Conclusion: COIN基准测试揭示了当前方法在交互推理能力上的关键局限性，为未来具身智能研究提供了系统评估框架和细粒度分析

Abstract: Generalist embodied agents must perform interactive, causally-dependent reasoning, continually interacting with the environment, acquiring information, and updating plans to solve long-horizon tasks before they could be adopted in real-life scenarios. For instance, retrieving an apple from a cabinet may require opening multiple doors and drawers before the apple becomes visible and reachable, demanding sequential interaction under partial observability. However, existing benchmarks fail to systematically evaluate this essential capability. We introduce COIN, a benchmark designed to assess interactive reasoning in realistic robotic manipulation through three key contributions. First, we construct COIN-50: 50 interactive tasks in daily scenarios, and create COIN-Primitive required by causally-dependent tasks, and COIN-Composition with mid-term complexity for skill learning and generalization evaluation. Second, we develop a low-cost mobile AR teleoperation system and collect the COIN-Primitive Dataset with 50 demonstrations per primitive task (1,000 in total). Third, we develop systematic evaluation metrics about execution stability and generalization robustness to evaluate CodeAsPolicy, VLA, and language-conditioned H-VLA approaches. Our comprehensive evaluation reveals critical limitations in current methods: models struggle with interactive reasoning tasks due to significant gaps between visual understanding and motor execution. We provide fine-grained analysis of these limitations.

</details>


### [66] [Time-Division Multiplexing Actuation in Tendon-Driven Arms: Lightweight Design and Fault Tolerance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16887)
*Shoujie Li,Changqing Guo,Jianle Xu,Hong Luo,Xueqian Wang,Wenbo Ding,Bin Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TDMA技术通过时分复用驱动显著减少肌腱驱动机器人执行器数量，同时保持高扭矩输出和固有容错能力，应用于MuxArm机械臂实现轻量化与高负载性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空航天应用中的机器人操作器需要在轻量化构造和容错操作之间取得平衡，以满足严格的重量限制并确保在远程危险环境中的可靠性。

Method: 提出时分复用驱动技术，采用垂直堆叠旋转选择结构，集成自旋转TDM电机、电磁离合器、蜗轮减速器和双编码器系统；开发驱动空间轨迹规划算法实现容错控制。

Result: MuxArm自重2.17kg，支持10kg驱动能力，末端执行器精度达其长度的1%；驱动空间轨迹规划算法相比传统方法减少肌腱负载达50%；在自由空间、杂乱和受限环境中均表现稳健。

Conclusion: TDMA为肌腱驱动机器人提供了一种实用的轻量化、高扭矩、容错驱动解决方案，通过硬件创新和算法优化实现了航空航天应用所需的性能平衡。

Abstract: Robotic manipulators for aerospace applications require a delicate balance between lightweight construction and fault-tolerant operation to satisfy strict weight limitations and ensure reliability in remote, hazardous environments. This paper presents Time-Division Multiplexing Actuation (TDMA), a practical approach for tendon-driven robots that significantly reduces actuator count while preserving high torque output and intrinsic fault tolerance. The key hardware employs a vertically-stacked rotational selection structure that integrates self-rotating TDM motors for rapid configuration, electromagnetic clutches enabling sub-0.1 second engagement, a worm gear reducer for enhanced load capacity and self-locking capability, and a dual-encoder system for precise, long-term positioning. Leveraging TDMA, the proposed MuxArm achieves a self-weight of 2.17 kg, supports an actuator driving capacity of 10 kg, and maintains end-effector accuracy up to 1% of its length, even under partial servo failure. Additionally, an actuation space trajectory planning algorithm is developed, enabling fault-tolerant control and reducing tendon load by up to 50% compared to conventional methods. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate MuxArm's robust performance in diverse settings, including free-space, cluttered, and confined environments.

</details>


### [67] [Leveraging VR Robot Games to Facilitate Data Collection for Embodied Intelligence Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16903)
*Yihan Zhang,Ziyun Huang,Linqi Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于Unity的VR游戏化数据收集框架，通过程序化场景生成、人形机器人控制、自动任务评估和轨迹记录，验证了虚拟环境作为具身数据收集的有效解决方案


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统具身交互数据收集成本高、难度大，受限于传统接口的可访问性，需要更高效、可扩展的数据收集方法

Method: 基于Unity开发游戏化数据收集框架，包含程序化场景生成、VR人形机器人控制、自动任务评估和轨迹记录；以垃圾拾取放置任务为原型验证完整工作流程

Result: 收集的演示数据展现了状态-动作空间的广泛覆盖；任务难度增加导致运动强度提高和手臂工作空间更广泛探索；框架证明游戏化虚拟环境是有效且可扩展的具身数据收集方案

Conclusion: 游戏化虚拟环境可作为具身数据收集的有效且可扩展解决方案，通过VR控制人形机器人结合程序化场景生成和自动评估，能够高效收集高质量交互数据

Abstract: Collecting embodied interaction data at scale remains costly and difficult due to the limited accessibility of conventional interfaces. We present a gamified data collection framework based on Unity that combines procedural scene generation, VR-based humanoid robot control, automatic task evaluation, and trajectory logging. A trash pick-and-place task prototype is developed to validate the full workflow.Experimental results indicate that the collected demonstrations exhibit broad coverage of the state-action space, and that increasing task difficulty leads to higher motion intensity as well as more extensive exploration of the arm's workspace. The proposed framework demonstrates that game-oriented virtual environments can serve as an effective and extensible solution for embodied data collection.

</details>


### [68] [NaviFormer: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Transformer-like Model to Holistically Solve the Navigation Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16967)
*Daniel Fuertes,Andrea Cavallaro,Carlos R. del-Blanco,Fernando Jaureguizar,Narciso García*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NaviFormer：基于Transformer的深度强化学习模型，通过同时预测高层路线和低层轨迹来解决全局导航问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的导航问题通常需要同时解决路线规划（高层）和路径规划（低层）子问题，但现有方法往往只关注其中一个层面，缺乏整体高效的方法

Method: 提出NaviFormer模型，基于Transformer架构的深度强化学习方法，能够同时预测高层路线（航点序列）和低层轨迹（避碰路径）

Result: 实验表明NaviFormer具有竞争力的准确性，能够理解各子问题的约束和困难并相应改进性能；其卓越的计算速度证明了适用于实时任务

Conclusion: NaviFormer为全局导航问题提供了一个整体解决方案，通过同时处理路线和路径规划，在准确性和计算效率方面表现出色，适合实时应用

Abstract: Path planning is usually solved by addressing either the (high-level) route planning problem (waypoint sequencing to achieve the final goal) or the (low-level) path planning problem (trajectory prediction between two waypoints avoiding collisions). However, real-world problems usually require simultaneous solutions to the route and path planning subproblems with a holistic and efficient approach. In this paper, we introduce NaviFormer, a deep reinforcement learning model based on a Transformer architecture that solves the global navigation problem by predicting both high-level routes and low-level trajectories. To evaluate NaviFormer, several experiments have been conducted, including comparisons with other algorithms. Results show competitive accuracy from NaviFormer since it can understand the constraints and difficulties of each subproblem and act consequently to improve performance. Moreover, its superior computation speed proves its suitability for real-time missions.

</details>


### [69] [Web-Gewu: A Browser-Based Interactive Playground for Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17050)
*Kaixuan Chen,Linqi Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Web-Gewu：基于WebRTC云-边-端协同架构的交互式机器人教育平台，通过边缘计算卸载物理仿真和RL训练，云端仅作信令中继，实现低成本浏览器P2P实时流式传输，降低机器人教育门槛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体智能快速发展背景下，机器人教育面临双重挑战：高计算门槛和繁琐环境配置。现有集中式云仿真方案GPU和带宽成本高昂难以大规模部署，纯本地计算受限于学习者硬件条件

Method: 提出Web-Gewu平台，基于WebRTC云-边-客户端协同架构：边缘节点负责所有物理仿真和强化学习训练，云端服务器仅作轻量级信令中继，实现低成本浏览器P2P实时流式传输。采用预定义鲁棒命令通信协议

Result: 学习者无需本地安装即可在浏览器中低延迟交互多形态机器人，同时实时可视化多维监控数据（包括RL奖励曲线）。系统提供高度可扩展、开箱即用、无障碍的教学基础设施

Conclusion: Web-Gewu显著降低了前沿机器人技术的入门门槛，为体智能教育提供了可大规模部署的解决方案，解决了现有方案的成本和可访问性问题

Abstract: With the rapid development of embodied intelligence, robotics education faces a dual challenge: high computational barriers and cumbersome environment configuration. Existing centralized cloud simulation solutions incur substantial GPU and bandwidth costs that preclude large-scale deployment, while pure local computing is severely constrained by learners' hardware limitations. To address these issues, we propose \href{http://47.76.242.88:8080/receiver/index.html}{Web-Gewu}, an interactive robotics education platform built on a WebRTC cloud-edge-client collaborative architecture. The system offloads all physics simulation and reinforcement learning (RL) training to the edge node, while the cloud server acts exclusively as a lightweight signaling relay, enabling extremely low-cost browser-based peer-to-peer (P2P) real-time streaming. Learners can interact with multi-form robots at low end-to-end latency directly in a web browser without any local installation, and simultaneously observe real-time visualization of multi-dimensional monitoring data, including reinforcement learning reward curves. Combined with a predefined robust command communication protocol, Web-Gewu provides a highly scalable, out-of-the-box, and barrier-free teaching infrastructure for embodied intelligence, significantly lowering the barrier to entry for cutting-edge robotics technology.

</details>


### [70] [Modeling, Control and Self-sensing of Dielectric Elastomer Soft Actuators: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17199)
*Y. Zhao,G. Meng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了介电弹性体致动器（DEAs）的建模、控制和自感知方法，分析了现有挑战并展望了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DEAs在软体机器人应用中具有轻量化、大应变、快速响应和高能量密度等优势，但由于非线性弹性、固有粘弹性蠕变、迟滞和振动动力学等特性，其建模、控制和自感知面临挑战，阻碍了实际应用。本文旨在系统梳理相关研究成果以推动DEAs的进一步发展。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，系统梳理了DEAs的物理建模与现象学建模方法、开环前馈控制、反馈控制、前馈-反馈控制和自适应前馈控制等控制策略，以及基于物理模型和数据驱动的自感知方法。

Result: 全面总结了DEAs在建模、控制和自感知方面的研究进展，对比了不同方法的优缺点，为研究人员提供了系统的技术路线图。

Conclusion: 虽然DEAs在软体机器人领域具有巨大潜力，但仍需解决非线性、迟滞等挑战。未来研究应关注更精确的建模方法、鲁棒控制策略和高效自感知技术，以推动DEAs的实际应用。

Abstract: Dielectric elastomer actuators (DEAs) have garnered extensive attention especially in soft robotic applications over the past few decades owing to the advantages of lightweight, large strain, fast response and high energy density. However, because the DEAs suffer from nonlinear elasticity, inherent viscoelastic creep, hysteresis and vibrational dynamics, the modeling, control and self-sensing of DEAs are challenging, thereby hindering the practical applications of DEAs. In order to address these challenges, numerous studies have been conducted. In this review, various physics-based modeling methods and phenomenological modeling methods for predicting the electromechanical response of DEAs are presented and discussed. Different control methods for DEAs are reviewed, which are classified into open-loop feedforward control, feedback control, feedforward-feedback control and adaptive feedforward control. Physics-based self-sensing methods and data-driven self-sensing methods for reconstructing the DEA displacement without the need for additional sensors are discussed. Finally, the existing problems and new opportunities for the further studies are summarized.

</details>


### [71] [Planning Smooth and Safe Control Laws for a Unicycle Robot Among Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17212)
*Aref Amiri,Basak Sakcak,Steven M. LaValle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个用于单轮点机器人在有障碍物环境中安全导航的框架，通过QP公式生成平滑向量场，设计非线性反馈控制器满足Nagumo定理条件，确保安全集前向不变性，无需在线优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决单轮点机器人在有障碍物环境中从几乎所有允许状态安全导航到目标位置的问题，同时考虑输入限制。现有方法可能无法保证安全集的前向不变性，或需要在线优化计算。

Method: 1. 引入新颖的QP公式生成C∞平滑向量场，减少总弯曲和总转向；2. 设计解析的非线性反馈控制器，满足Nagumo定理条件，确保安全集前向不变性；3. 控制器无需在线优化，能处理硬输入限制。

Result: 控制器即使在硬输入限制下也能安全收敛到目标位置。仿真显示，与基线方法相比，到达时间快两倍，角控制努力降低超过50%。

Conclusion: 提出的框架为单轮机器人提供了安全导航解决方案，通过平滑向量场和非线性反馈控制器实现了高效、低控制努力的安全导航，无需在线优化计算。

Abstract: This paper presents a framework for safe navigation of a unicycle point robot to a goal position in an environment populated with obstacles from almost any admissible state, considering input limits. We introduce a novel QP formulation to create a Cinfinity-smooth vector field with reduced total bending and total turning. Then we design an analytic, non-linear feedback controller that inherently satisfies the conditions of Nagumo's theorem, ensuring forward invariance of the safe set without requiring any online optimization. We have demonstrated that our controller, even under hard input limits, safely converges to the goal position. Simulations confirm the effectiveness of the proposed framework, resulting in a twice faster arrival time with over 50\% lower angular control effort compared to the baseline.

</details>


### [72] [GaLa: Hypergraph-Guided Visual Language Models for Procedural Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17241)
*Kun Wang,Yiming Li,Mingcheng Qu,Aqiang Zhang,Guang Yang,Tonghua Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaLa是一个用于多模态程序规划的视觉语言框架，通过超图表示和TriView超图编码器，显式捕捉对象间的隐式空间关系和深层语义结构，显著提升执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法过度依赖视觉语言模型自身的推理能力，忽视了可以从多模态输入中挖掘的丰富结构化语义信息，导致模型难以有效理解复杂场景中的功能空间关系。

Method: 提出基于超图的表示方法：将图像中的对象实例建模为节点，根据对象属性和功能语义聚合对象构建区域级超边；设计TriView超图编码器，通过对比学习在节点视图、区域视图和节点-区域关联视图之间强制语义一致性。

Result: 在ActPlan1K和ALFRED基准测试上的广泛实验表明，GaLa在执行成功率、LCS和规划正确性方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: GaLa通过超图表示和跨视图语义一致性学习，有效利用多模态数据中的隐式空间关系和深层语义结构，提升了具身AI系统中程序规划的性能。

Abstract: Implicit spatial relations and deep semantic structures encoded in object attributes are crucial for procedural planning in embodied AI systems. However, existing approaches often over rely on the reasoning capabilities of vision language models (VLMs) themselves, while overlooking the rich structured semantic information that can be mined from multimodal inputs. As a result, models struggle to effectively understand functional spatial relationships in complex scenes. To fully exploit implicit spatial relations and deep semantic structures in multimodal data, we propose GaLa, a vision language framework for multimodal procedural planning. GaLa introduces a hypergraph-based representation, where object instances in the image are modeled as nodes, and region-level hyperedges are constructed by aggregating objects according to their attributes and functional semantics. This design explicitly captures implicit semantic relations among objects as well as the hierarchical organization of functional regions. Furthermore, we design a TriView HyperGraph Encoder that enforces semantic consistency across the node view, area view, and node area association view via contrastive learning, enabling hypergraph semantics to be more effectively injected into downstream VLM reasoning. Extensive experiments on the ActPlan1K and ALFRED benchmarks demonstrate that GaLa significantly outperforms existing methods in terms of execution success rate, LCS, and planning correctness.

</details>


### [73] [MM-Hand: A 21-DOF Multi-modal Modular Dexterous Robotic Hand with Remote Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17245)
*Zhuoheng Li,Qingquan Lin,Checheng Yu,Qiangyu Chen,Zhiqian Lan,Lutong Zhang,Hongyang Li,Ping Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MM-Hand是一个基于远程肌腱驱动的21自由度多模态模块化灵巧手，具有轻量化、传感器丰富和易于维护的特点，所有硬件设计和软件框架均已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高自由度灵巧手设计占用宝贵的手部空间、增加末端执行器质量且存在热量积累问题。远程肌腱驱动可将电机移至机器人基座或外部电机中心，为手指和手掌释放空间，容纳更多自由度、传感模块和可维护机械结构。

Method: 采用远程肌腱驱动方案，设计弹簧回位肌腱驱动手指、模块化3D打印手指和手掌结构、快速肌腱连接器便于维护，集成多模态传感系统（关节角度传感器、触觉传感器、电机侧反馈、手掌内立体视觉），分析肌腱-鞘长度变化和摩擦损耗以指导路由、电机中心和闭环关节控制设计。

Result: 实验验证了系统的传动、输出力、传感和控制能力：在1米远程鞘传输下指尖力达到25N，展示了长距离肌腱路由下的实际负载能力；闭环关节级实验评估了静态臂和臂运动期间的指令跟踪性能。

Conclusion: MM-Hand为灵巧操作研究提供了一个轻量化、传感器丰富且易于维护的硬件平台，所有硬件设计和软件框架已完全开源，支持社区研究。

Abstract: High-DOF dexterous hands require compact actuation, rich sensing, and reliable thermal behavior, but conventional designs often occupy valuable in-hand space, increase end-effector mass, and suffer from heat accumulation near the hand. Remote tendon-driven actuation offers an alternative by relocating motors to the robot base or an external motor hub, thereby freeing the fingers and palm for additional degrees of freedom, sensing modules, and maintainable mechanical structures. This paper presents MM-Hand, a 21-DOF Multimodal Modular dexterous hand based on remote tendon-driven actuation. The hand integrates spring-return tendon-driven fingers, modular 3D-printed finger and palm structures, quick tendon connectors for maintenance, and a multimodal sensing system including joint angle sensors, tactile sensors, motor-side feedback, and in-palm stereo vision. We further analyze tendon-sheath length variation and friction loss to guide the design of the routing, motor hub, and closed-loop joint control. Experiments validate the transmission, output force, sensing, and control capability of the system. The fingertip force reaches 25N under a 1m remote sheath transmission, demonstrating practical load capacity despite long-distance tendon routing. Closed-loop joint-level experiments further evaluate command tracking with a static arm and during arm motion. These results show that MM-Hand provides a lightweight, sensor-rich, and maintainable hardware platform for dexterous manipulation research. To support the community, all hardware designs and software frameworks are made fully open-source at https://mmlab.hk/research/MM-Hand.

</details>


### [74] [A Rapid Deployment Pipeline for Autonomous Humanoid Grasping Based on Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17258)
*Yifei Yan,Yankai Liao,Linqi Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个端到端快速部署流水线，将人形机器人操纵新物体的部署时间从1-2天缩短到约30分钟，通过集成三个基础模型组件实现快速物体检测、3D重建和姿态跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人形机器人操纵新物体需要1-2天的部署时间，包括数据收集、手动标注、3D模型获取和模型训练，过程繁琐且耗时。需要一种快速部署方案来降低人形机器人应用的准入门槛。

Method: 集成三个基础模型组件：1) 基于Roboflow的自动标注辅助训练YOLOv8物体检测器；2) 基于Meta SAM 3D的3D重建，无需专用激光扫描仪；3) 基于FoundationPose的零样本6自由度姿态跟踪，直接使用SAM 3D生成的网格作为模板。姿态估计驱动基于Unity的逆运动学规划器，通过UDP将关节命令流式传输到Unitree G1人形机器人并通过Unitree SDK执行。

Result: 检测精度达到mAP@0.5 = 0.995，姿态跟踪精度σ < 1.05 mm，在真实机器人工作空间内五个位置成功完成抓取。进一步验证了流水线在汽车车窗涂胶任务上的通用性。

Conclusion: 将感知基础模型与日常成像设备（如智能手机）相结合，可以显著降低人形机器人操纵任务的部署门槛，实现约30分钟的快速部署，相比传统方法大幅提升效率。

Abstract: Deploying a humanoid robot to manipulate a new object has traditionally required one to two days of effort: data collection, manual annotation, 3D model acquisition, and model training. This paper presents an end-to-end rapid deployment pipeline that integrates three foundation-model components to shorten the onboarding cycle for a new object to approximately 30 minutes: (i) Roboflow-based automatic annotation to assist in training a YOLOv8 object detector; (ii) 3D reconstruction based on Meta SAM 3D, which eliminates the need for a dedicated laser scanner; and (iii) zero-shot 6-DoF pose tracking based on FoundationPose, using the SAM~3D-generated mesh directly as the template. The estimated pose drives a Unity-based inverse kinematics planner, whose joint commands are streamed via UDP to a Unitree~G1 humanoid and executed through the Unitree SDK. We demonstrate detection accuracy of mAP@0.5 = 0.995, pose tracking precision of $σ< 1.05$ mm, and successful grasping on a real robot at five positions within the workspace. We further verify the generality of the pipeline on an automobile-window glue-application task. The results show that combining foundation models for perception with everyday imaging devices (e.g., smartphones) can substantially lower the deployment barrier for humanoid manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [75] [Learning Whole-Body Humanoid Locomotion via Motion Generation and Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17335)
*Zewei Zhang,Kehan Wen,Michael Xu,Junzhe He,Chenhao Li,Takahiro Miki,Clemens Schwarke,Chong Zhang,Xue Bin Peng,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结合扩散模型生成地形感知参考运动与强化学习跟踪器的全身人形机器人运动框架，支持基于感知的实时地形适应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决全身人形机器人运动控制的高维性、形态不稳定性和实时地形适应需求。传统强化学习奖励塑形方法通常导致下半身主导行为，而基于模仿的学习方法虽然能学习协调的全身技能，但缺乏从感知在线适应参考运动以实现地形感知运动的能力。

Method: 1) 在重定向的人类运动数据上训练扩散模型，用于实时预测地形感知参考运动；2) 使用该运动数据通过强化学习训练全身参考跟踪器；3) 在闭环设置中，使用冻结的运动生成器对跟踪器进行微调，以提高在不完美生成参考下的鲁棒性。

Result: 系统支持具有地形感知全身适应的定向目标到达控制，可在Unitree G1人形机器人上部署，配备板载感知和计算。硬件实验成功穿越箱子、障碍物、楼梯和混合地形组合。定量结果显示在线运动生成和运动跟踪器微调对提高泛化性和鲁棒性的益处。

Conclusion: 提出的框架成功结合了从参考运动学习的技能与地形感知适应能力，实现了在复杂地形上的鲁棒全身人形机器人运动控制，解决了传统方法在实时地形适应方面的局限性。

Abstract: Whole-body humanoid locomotion is challenging due to high-dimensional control, morphological instability, and the need for real-time adaptation to various terrains using onboard perception. Directly applying reinforcement learning (RL) with reward shaping to humanoid locomotion often leads to lower-body-dominated behaviors, whereas imitation-based RL can learn more coordinated whole-body skills but is typically limited to replaying reference motions without a mechanism to adapt them online from perception for terrain-aware locomotion. To address this gap, we propose a whole-body humanoid locomotion framework that combines skills learned from reference motions with terrain-aware adaptation. We first train a diffusion model on retargeted human motions for real-time prediction of terrain-aware reference motions. Concurrently, we train a whole-body reference tracker with RL using this motion data. To improve robustness under imperfectly generated references, we further fine-tune the tracker with a frozen motion generator in a closed-loop setting. The resulting system supports directional goal-reaching control with terrain-aware whole-body adaptation, and can be deployed on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot with onboard perception and computation. The hardware experiments demonstrate successful traversal over boxes, hurdles, stairs, and mixed terrain combinations. Quantitative results further show the benefits of incorporating online motion generation and fine-tuning the motion tracker for improved generalization and robustness.

</details>


### [76] [Think before Go: Hierarchical Reasoning for Image-goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17407)
*Pengna Li,Kangyi Wu,Shaoqing Xu,Fang Li,Lin Zhao,Long Chen,Zhi-Xin Yang,Nanning Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HRNav：一种分层推理导航框架，将图像目标导航分解为高级规划和低级执行，使用视觉语言模型生成短期计划，结合强化学习策略执行动作，并引入游荡抑制惩罚来减少徘徊问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端导航方法在目标距离较远或位于其他房间时，难以提取有效的视觉线索，导致智能体徘徊。受人类认知原则启发，复杂任务中深思熟虑的高级推理能够指导快速反应性执行。

Method: 提出分层推理导航框架：1）高级规划：在自收集数据集上训练视觉语言模型，生成短期计划（如"穿过门"或"沿走廊走"）；2）低级执行：使用在线强化学习策略，基于短期计划决定动作；3）引入游荡抑制惩罚进一步减少徘徊问题。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验证明了该方法的优越性，能够有效处理远距离目标和跨房间导航任务。

Conclusion: HRNav通过分层分解导航任务，将长视野任务简化为更易处理的短期计划，结合强化学习执行，有效解决了图像目标导航中的徘徊问题，在复杂环境中表现出色。

Abstract: Image-goal navigation steers an agent to a target location specified by an image in unseen environments. Existing methods primarily handle this task by learning an end-to-end navigation policy, which compares the similarities of target and observation images and directly predicts the actions. However, when the target is distant or lies in another room, such methods fail to extract informative visual cues, leading the agent to wander around. Motivated by the human cognitive principle that deliberate, high-level reasoning guides fast, reactive execution in complex tasks, we propose Hierarchical Reasoning Navigation (HRNav), a framework that decomposes image-goal navigation into high-level planning and low-level execution. In high-level planning, a vision-language model is trained on a self-collected dataset to generate a short-horizon plan, such as whether the agent should walk through the door or down the hallway. This downgrades the difficulty of the long-horizon task, making it more amenable to the execution part. In low-level execution, an online reinforcement learning policy is utilized to decide actions conditioned on the short-horizon plan. We also devise a novel Wandering Suppression Penalty (WSP) to further reduce the wandering problem. Together, these components form a hierarchical framework for Image-Goal Navigation. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate the superiority of our method.

</details>


### [77] [FLASH: Fast Learning via GPU-Accelerated Simulation for High-Fidelity Deformable Manipulation in Minutes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17513)
*Siyuan Luo,Bingyang Zhou,Chong Zhang,Xin Liu,Zhenhao Huang,Gang Yang,Zhengtao Han,Xiaotian Hu,Eric Yang,Rymon Yu,Ziqiu Zeng,Fan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLASH是一个GPU原生的仿真框架，专门用于接触丰富的可变形物体操作，通过重新设计物理引擎实现GPU并行化，能够高效模拟超过300万自由度，支持仅用合成数据训练的策略实现零样本仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真框架（如Isaac Sim）在刚体操作和运动控制方面表现良好，但在可变形物体操作方面存在瓶颈。可变形材料的几何形状持续变化，加上大量顶点和接触约束，难以实现大规模交互学习所需的高精度、速度和稳定性。

Method: FLASH是一个基于GPU原生的仿真框架，采用精确的NCP（非线性互补问题）求解器，强制执行严格的接触和变形约束。框架从底层重新设计物理引擎以充分利用现代GPU架构，包括优化的碰撞处理和内存布局，而非简单移植传统的SIMD求解器到GPU。

Result: FLASH在单个RTX 5090 GPU上能够以30 FPS的速度模拟超过300万自由度，同时准确模拟物理交互。仅使用FLASH生成的合成数据在几分钟内训练的策略实现了鲁棒的零样本仿真到现实迁移，在物理机器人上成功完成了毛巾折叠和衣物折叠等挑战性任务，无需任何真实世界演示。

Conclusion: FLASH为接触丰富的可变形物体操作提供了一个高效、可扩展的仿真解决方案，能够显著加速机器人学习过程，为劳动密集型的真实世界数据收集提供了实用的替代方案，推动了可变形物体操作研究的进展。

Abstract: Simulation frameworks such as Isaac Sim have enabled scalable robot learning for locomotion and rigid-body manipulation; however, contact-rich simulation remains a major bottleneck for deformable object manipulation.
